
================================================================================
research/early_planetary_comparison/Jovian_Venus_021126_Submission_Package/Research_Master/02_Model_Code/atomic_io.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import os,io
def put(dest,data):
 dest=Path(dest);temp=dest.with_name(dest.name+'.pending')
 with temp.open('wb') as f:
  f.write(data);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())
 temp.replace(dest)
 assert dest.stat().st_size==len(data)
def install():
 import pandas as pd
 original=pd.DataFrame.to_csv
 def atomic(self,path_or_buf=None,*args,**kwargs):
  if not isinstance(path_or_buf,(str,Path)):return original(self,path_or_buf,*args,**kwargs)
  data=original(self,None,*args,**kwargs).encode();put(path_or_buf,data)
 pd.DataFrame.to_csv=atomic
 import matplotlib.figure
 save=matplotlib.figure.Figure.savefig
 def savefig(self,fname,*args,**kwargs):
  if not isinstance(fname,(str,Path)):return save(self,fname,*args,**kwargs)
  buf=io.BytesIO();kw=dict(kwargs);kw.setdefault('format',Path(fname).suffix.lstrip('.'));save(self,buf,*args,**kw);put(fname,buf.getvalue())
 matplotlib.figure.Figure.savefig=savefig
 write_text=Path.write_text
 def text(self,data,encoding=None,errors=None,newline=None):
  if newline is not None:data=data.replace('\n',newline)
  put(self,data.encode(encoding or 'utf-8',errors or 'strict'));return len(data)
 Path.write_text=text


================================================================================
research/early_planetary_comparison/Jovian_Venus_021126_Submission_Package/Research_Master/02_Model_Code/validate.py
================================================================================
from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd,json,zipfile,hashlib
from PIL import Image
import fitz
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];D=P/'03_Data';e=pd.read_csv(D/'planet_water_atmosphere_habitability_ensemble.csv');checks={}
def check(k,v):
 checks[k]=bool(v)
 if not v:raise AssertionError(k)
check('planet_rows_18432',len(e)==18432)
check('water_total_mass_balance',np.allclose(e.initial_retained_water_GEL_m,e.bound_water_GEL_m+e.escaped_water_GEL_m+e.unbound_water_GEL_m+e.remaining_deep_water_GEL_m,rtol=1e-12,atol=1e-10))
check('finite_deep_reserve_release',np.allclose(e.initial_deep_water_GEL_m,e.remaining_deep_water_GEL_m+e.outgassed_water_GEL_m))
check('accessible_initial_plus_deep_equals_total',np.allclose(e.initial_accessible_water_GEL_m+e.initial_deep_water_GEL_m,e.initial_retained_water_GEL_m))
check('free_water_compartments',np.allclose(e.unbound_water_GEL_m,e.vapor_water_GEL_m+e.surface_condensed_GEL_m+e.pore_water_GEL_m))
check('suitable_le_liquid_le_pore',((e.suitable_water_GEL_m<=e.liquid_0_80_GEL_m+1e-9)&(e.liquid_0_80_GEL_m<=e.pore_water_GEL_m+1e-9)).all())
check('gas_mole_fraction_closure',np.allclose(e[['x_CO2','x_N2','x_H2','x_H2O']].sum(axis=1),1))
check('eligible_temperature_bounds',e.mean_water_C.dropna().between(0,80).all())
check('hot_Venus_has_no_suitable_volume',e[e.branch.eq('Venus_hot')].suitable_water_m3.eq(0).all())
check('identical_Earth_Venus_radioactive_heat',np.allclose(e[e.branch.eq('Earth')].radiogenic_power_W.to_numpy(),e[e.branch.eq('Venus_wet')].radiogenic_power_W.to_numpy()))
kt=pd.read_csv(D/'transfer_kernel_provenance.csv');check('uncalibrated_routes_remain_missing',kt[kt.calibration.eq('unconstrained')].p_dynamic_10Myr.isna().all())
check('Worth_percent_to_fraction_Europa',np.isclose(kt[(kt.origin=='Mars')&(kt.destination=='Europa')].p_dynamic_10Myr.iloc[0],2.7e-8,atol=1e-15))
check('mixing_rows_55296',len(pd.read_csv(D/'rotation_tidal_mixing_precursors.csv'))==55296)
check('exchange_rows_1440',len(pd.read_csv(D/'conditional_exchange_rates.csv'))==1440)
for p in P.rglob('*.docx'):
 with zipfile.ZipFile(p) as z:check('Word_CRC_'+p.stem,z.testzip() is None)
for p in (P/'04_Figures').glob('*.png'):
 Image.open(p).verify();checks['Figure_PNG_'+p.stem]=True
for p in P.rglob('*.pdf'):
 doc=fitz.open(p);check('PDF_'+p.stem,len(doc)>0)
checks['source_files_unchanged_note']='Previous Mars CSV and Archer article retained as supplied; new model changes are isolated.'
report={'scope':'Reproducible controlled sensitivity study, not historical validation','checks':checks,'planet_rows':len(e),'draws':384,'age_horizons':12,'document_count':len(list(P.rglob('*.docx'))),'figure_count':len(list((P/'04_Figures').glob('*.png'))),'all_assertions_passed':True}
(P/'Validation_Report.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(json.dumps(report,indent=2))


================================================================================
research/early_planetary_comparison/Jovian_Venus_021126_Submission_Package/Recovered_Validation_Extension/Validation_Extension/run_validation.py
================================================================================
import numpy as np, pandas as pd, pathlib, matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.stats import beta
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
(P/'Data').mkdir(exist_ok=True);(P/'Figures').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
AU=1.495978707e11; DAY=86400.; MU=1.32712440018e20
radii={'Venus':0.723,'Earth':1.0,'Mars':1.524,'Jovian_orbit':5.204}
# Simplified test-particle heliocentric 2D/3D Kepler model; circular coplanar planets, fixed Sun, no mutual perturbations.
# Trajectories integrate using velocity Verlet; launch from orbit with isotropic ejection velocity 1-8 km/s.
# A 'close approach' is NOT an impact, capture, transfer or viable inoculation.
rng=np.random.default_rng(20261102)
N=1500; dt=10*DAY; steps=int(100*365.25/10)
rows=[]; samples=[]
for src,dst in [('Mars','Earth'),('Mars','Venus'),('Earth','Venus'),('Earth','Mars'),('Mars','Jovian_orbit'),('Jovian_orbit','Venus')]:
    a=radii[src]*AU; b=radii[dst]*AU
    phi=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,N); theta=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,N); u=rng.uniform(-1,1,N)
    speed=rng.uniform(1e3,8e3,N); s=np.sqrt(1-u*u)
    p=np.column_stack((a*np.cos(phi),a*np.sin(phi),np.zeros(N)))
    v=np.column_stack((-np.sin(phi),np.cos(phi),np.zeros(N)))*np.sqrt(MU/a)
    v+=speed[:,None]*np.column_stack((s*np.cos(theta),s*np.sin(theta),u))
    phase_target=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,N)
    min_dist=np.full(N,np.inf); min_t=np.full(N,np.nan)
    acc=-MU*p/np.linalg.norm(p,axis=1)[:,None]**3
    for j in range(steps):
        p+=v*dt+0.5*acc*dt*dt
        acc_new=-MU*p/np.linalg.norm(p,axis=1)[:,None]**3
        v+=0.5*(acc+acc_new)*dt;acc=acc_new
        t=(j+1)*dt
        angle=phase_target+np.sqrt(MU/b**3)*t
        target=np.column_stack((b*np.cos(angle),b*np.sin(angle),np.zeros(N)))
        dist=np.linalg.norm(p-target,axis=1)
        mask=dist<min_dist;min_dist[mask]=dist[mask];min_t[mask]=t/(365.25*DAY)
    for threshold in [0.01,0.05]:
        hits=min_dist<threshold*AU; k=int(hits.sum()); low,high=beta.ppf([.025,.975],k+.5,N-k+.5)
        rows.append(dict(source=src,destination=dst,threshold_AU=threshold,n=N,encounters=k,encounter_fraction=k/N,CI95_low=low,CI95_high=high,median_time_years=float(np.median(min_t[hits])) if k else np.nan))
    samples.extend(dict(source=src,destination=dst,min_distance_AU=round(float(d/AU),6),time_of_min_distance_years=round(float(t),3)) for d,t in zip(min_dist,min_t))
pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_orbital_encounters.csv',index=False)
pd.DataFrame(samples).to_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_trajectory_minima.csv',index=False)
# survival toy model: exp(-dose/D37) * exp(-time/tau); independently sweep assumptions.
# dose rate in Gy/year, shielding attenuation 0.001..1, D37=100..10000 Gy; tau=1e3..1e7 yr
sr=[]
for transit in [1e2,1e4,1e6,1e7]:
 for rate in [0.01,0.1,1.0]:
  for atten in [0.001,0.01,0.1,1.0]:
   for d37 in [100,1000,10000]:
    for tau in [1e3,1e5,1e7]:
     dose=transit*rate*atten
     survival=np.exp(-dose/d37-transit/tau)
     sr.append(dict(transit_years=transit,dose_rate_Gy_per_year=rate,shielding_transmission=atten,D37_Gy=d37,nonradiation_tau_years=tau,assumed_survival_fraction=survival))
pd.DataFrame(sr).to_csv(P/'Data'/'survival_parameter_grid.csv',index=False)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(9,5))
for row in rows:
 if row['threshold_AU']==.01:
  label=row['source']+' → '+row['destination'];ax.errorbar(label,row['encounter_fraction'],yerr=[[max(0,row['encounter_fraction']-row['CI95_low'])],[max(0,row['CI95_high']-row['encounter_fraction'])]],fmt='o',capsize=4)
ax.set_ylabel('Fraction with encounter within 0.01 AU');ax.set_title('100-year circular-orbit test-particle proxy (not collision probability)');ax.tick_params(axis='x',rotation=35);ax.grid(axis='y',alpha=.25);fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'proxy_encounters.png',dpi=180);fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'proxy_encounters.svg');plt.close(fig)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(8,5))
for atten in [.001,.01,.1,1.]:
 times=np.logspace(2,7,200); rate=.1;D37=1000;tau=1e6
 ax.plot(times,np.exp(-times*rate*atten/D37-times/tau),label=f'Shield transmission {atten:g}')
ax.set(xscale='log',yscale='log',xlabel='Transit time (years)',ylabel='Assumed viable fraction',title='Illustrative radiation + nonradiation survival (not empirical)');ax.set_ylim(1e-15,1.2);ax.legend();ax.grid(alpha=.2);fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'survival_sensitivity.png',dpi=180);fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'survival_sensitivity.svg');plt.close(fig)
print(pd.DataFrame(rows).to_string(index=False))

research/early_planetary_comparison/Jovian_Venus_021126_Submission_Package/Recovered_Validation_Extension/Validation_Extension/build_report.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd, shutil, zipfile
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate,Paragraph,Spacer,Table,TableStyle,Image,PageBreak
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet
from reportlab.lib import colors
from reportlab.lib.units import inch
from docx import Document
from docx.shared import Inches
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parent; out=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Validation');out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
base=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Expanded.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(base) as z:z.extractall(out)
new=out/'Validation_Extension';new.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
for name in ['Data','Figures']:
 shutil.copytree(P/name,new/name,dirs_exist_ok=True)
shutil.copy2(P/'run_validation.py',new/'run_validation.py')
shutil.copy2(P/'build_report.py',new/'build_report.py')
df=pd.read_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_orbital_encounters.csv');short=df[df.threshold_AU==.01]
sections=[('Scope and model provenance','This is a new reproducible numerical sensitivity exercise. It is NOT independent validation of the original historical Mars–Earth–Venus habitability calculations, which require original forcing data, calibration, and independent replication.'),('Orbital approximation','1,500 Monte Carlo heliocentric trajectories per route, 100 years, 10-day velocity-Verlet time step, fixed solar gravity, circular coplanar target orbits, isotropic 1–8 km/s velocity increments. The Jovian destination is a circular 5.204 AU point proxy, not Europa, Ganymede or Callisto. No gravitational focusing, giant planet perturbations, resonances, impact cross-sections, ejection mass or actual satellite capture.'),('Interpretation','Fractions measure a minimum distance less than a prescribed threshold, not impact probability. Finite step size can miss close passages; trajectories can be numerically inaccurate at small perihelia. The 100-year horizon is vastly shorter than many lithopanspermia scenarios. The interval estimates describe Monte Carlo counting uncertainty conditional on this toy model, not structural or historical uncertainty.'),('Biological survival','Illustrative assumed surviving fraction = exp[-(transit_years × radiation_dose_rate_Gy_per_year × shielding_transmission / D37_Gy) - transit_years/nonradiation_tau_years]. Parameters are not inferred from microbes or actual rock dose-depth profiles. There is no ejection-shock, atmospheric-entry, ocean-delivery or ecological-establishment term.'),('Independent validation still required','(1) Reconstruct original geophysical and water-budget time series and reproduce every manuscript figure from source data. (2) Integrate early Solar System N-body dynamics with planetary migration, impactor populations, realistic ejecta speeds, long integration windows, Jovian satellites and encounter-to-impact mapping. (3) Fit survival and shielding models to measured organism and rock-radiation experiments and couple launch, transit, entry and establishment. (4) Propagate joint parameter uncertainty and compare competing origins hypotheses with explicit likelihoods. These tasks cannot be claimed complete from this simplified simulation.'),('Scientific conclusion','This experiment establishes a reproducible computational baseline and parameter sensitivities, but neither verifies early Jovian-to-Venus panspermia nor determines which planet hosted life first.')]
rows=[['Route','N','<0.01 AU','95% count interval']]
for r in short.itertuples():rows.append([f'{r.source} to {r.destination}',str(r.n),f'{r.encounter_fraction:.3%}',f'{r.CI95_low:.3%} to {r.CI95_high:.3%}'])
styles=getSampleStyleSheet();pdf=new/'Validation_Report.pdf';story=[Paragraph('Orbital and Biological Sensitivity Extension',styles['Title']),Paragraph('Jovian–Venus project 021126 | 2 November 2026',styles['Normal']),Spacer(1,14)]
for title,body in sections[:2]:story.extend([Paragraph(title,styles['Heading2']),Paragraph(body,styles['BodyText']),Spacer(1,10)])
t=Table(rows,colWidths=[170,45,85,150],repeatRows=1);t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#e6edf5')),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.3,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),8),('BOTTOMPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),7)]));story.append(t);story.append(Spacer(1,12))
for title,body in sections[2:]:story.extend([Paragraph(title,styles['Heading2']),Paragraph(body,styles['BodyText']),Spacer(1,10)])
story.append(PageBreak())
for img in ['proxy_encounters.png','survival_sensitivity.png']:
 story.append(Image(str(P/'Figures'/img),width=6.4*inch,height=3.55*inch));story.append(Spacer(1,15))
SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf),pagesize=(612,792),rightMargin=40,leftMargin=40,topMargin=40,bottomMargin=40).build(story)
doc=Document();doc.add_heading('Orbital and Biological Sensitivity Extension',0);doc.add_paragraph('Jovian–Venus project 021126 | 2 November 2026')
for title,body in sections[:2]:doc.add_heading(title,2);doc.add_paragraph(body)
t=doc.add_table(rows=0, cols=4);t.style='Light Shading Accent 1'
for rr in rows:
 c=t.add_row().cells
 for i,v in enumerate(rr):c[i].text=v
for title,body in sections[2:]:doc.add_heading(title,2);doc.add_paragraph(body)
for img in ['proxy_encounters.png','survival_sensitivity.png']:doc.add_picture(str(P/'Figures'/img),width=Inches(6.1))
doc.save(new/'Validation_Report.docx')
(new/'README.md').write_text('VALIDATION EXTENSION — reproducible but NOT independently validated historical panspermia research.\n\nRun: python run_validation.py\nRequires numpy pandas scipy matplotlib. Random seed 20261102.\nSource: original project materials retained in accompanying archive.\nCaution: no real N-body Jovian satellite transfers; no experimentally calibrated microbial survival.\n')
zipname=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Validation_Extended.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(zipname,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
 for f in out.rglob('*'):
  if f.is_file():z.write(f,f.relative_to(out))
print(zipname,zipname.stat().st_size)


================================================================================
research/early_planetary_comparison/Jovian_Venus_021126_Submission_Package/Recovered_Validation_Extension/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126/Code/reproduce_endpoints.py
================================================================================
"""Reproduce plots and checks from the published manuscript's stated reference endpoints.
These are manuscript-transcribed scenario outputs, NOT independently regenerated physics.
Run: python Code/reproduce_endpoints.py from project root.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
rows=list(csv.DictReader((root/'Data/reference_endpoints.csv').open()))
fig, ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(8,5))
for planet in ['Earth','Mars','Venus wet','Venus hot']:
    sub=[r for r in rows if r['branch']==planet]
    ax.plot([float(r['age_ga']) for r in sub],[float(r['habitable_gel_m']) for r in sub],marker='o',label=planet)
ax.set_xlabel('Age (Ga before present)')
ax.set_ylabel('Modeled 0–80 °C suitable-equivalent water (m GEL)')
ax.set_title('Manuscript endpoint comparison (not full time series)')
ax.invert_xaxis();ax.legend();fig.tight_layout()
fig.savefig(root/'Figures/endpoint_comparison.png',dpi=220)
plt.close(fig)
print('Saved endpoint comparison; intermediate ages and full model not reconstructed.')

research/validation_extension/Validation_Extension/run_validation.py

import numpy as np, pandas as pd, pathlib, matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.stats import beta
P=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent
(P/'Data').mkdir(exist_ok=True);(P/'Figures').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
AU=1.495978707e11; DAY=86400.; MU=1.32712440018e20

    samples.extend(dict(source=src,destination=dst,min_distance_AU=round(float(d/AU),6),time_of_min_distance_years=round(float(t),3)) for d,t in zip(min_dist,min_t))
pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_orbital_encounters.csv',index=False)
pd.DataFrame(samples).to_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_trajectory_minima.csv',index=False)
# survival toy model: exp(-dose/D37) * exp(-time/tau); independently sweep assumptions.

pd.DataFrame(sr).to_csv(P/'Data'/'survival_parameter_grid.csv',index=False)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(9,5))
for row in rows:
 if row['threshold_AU']==.01:
  label=row['source']+' → '+row['destination'];ax.errorbar(label,row['encounter_fraction'],yerr=[[max(0,row['encounter_fraction']-row['CI95_low'])],[max(0,row['CI95_high']-row['encounter_fraction'])]],fmt='o',capsize=4)

for atten in [.001,.01,.1,1.]:
 times=np.logspace(2,7,200); rate=.1;D37=1000;tau=1e6
 ax.plot(times,np.exp(-times*rate*atten/D37-times/tau),label=f'Shield transmission {atten:g}')
ax.set(xscale='log',yscale='log',xlabel='Transit time (years)',ylabel='Assumed viable fraction',title='Illustrative radiation + nonradiation survival (not empirical)');ax.set_ylim(1e-15,1.2);ax.legend();ax.grid(alpha=.2);fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'survival_sensitivity.png',dpi=180);fig.savefig(P/'Figures'/'survival_sensitivity.svg');plt.close(fig)
print(pd.DataFrame(rows).to_string(index=False))


================================================================================
research/validation_extension/Validation_Extension/build_report.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd, shutil, zipfile
from reportlab.platypus import SimpleDocTemplate,Paragraph,Spacer,Table,TableStyle,Image,PageBreak
from reportlab.lib.styles import getSampleStyleSheet
from reportlab.lib import colors
from reportlab.lib.units import inch
from docx import Document
from docx.shared import Inches
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parent; out=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Validation');out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
base=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Expanded.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(base) as z:z.extractall(out)
new=out/'Validation_Extension';new.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
for name in ['Data','Figures']:
 shutil.copytree(P/name,new/name,dirs_exist_ok=True)
shutil.copy2(P/'run_validation.py',new/'run_validation.py')
shutil.copy2(P/'build_report.py',new/'build_report.py')
df=pd.read_csv(P/'Data'/'proxy_orbital_encounters.csv');short=df[df.threshold_AU==.01]
sections=[('Scope and model provenance','This is a new reproducible numerical sensitivity exercise. It is NOT independent validation of the original historical Mars–Earth–Venus habitability calculations, which require original forcing data, calibration, and independent replication.'),('Orbital approximation','1,500 Monte Carlo heliocentric trajectories per route, 100 years, 10-day velocity-Verlet time step, fixed solar gravity, circular coplanar target orbits, isotropic 1–8 km/s velocity increments. The Jovian destination is a circular 5.204 AU point proxy, not Europa, Ganymede or Callisto. No gravitational focusing, giant planet perturbations, resonances, impact cross-sections, ejection mass or actual satellite capture.'),('Interpretation','Fractions measure a minimum distance less than a prescribed threshold, not impact probability. Finite step size can miss close passages; trajectories can be numerically inaccurate at small perihelia. The 100-year horizon is vastly shorter than many lithopanspermia scenarios. The interval estimates describe Monte Carlo counting uncertainty conditional on this toy model, not structural or historical uncertainty.'),('Biological survival','Illustrative assumed surviving fraction = exp[-(transit_years × radiation_dose_rate_Gy_per_year × shielding_transmission / D37_Gy) - transit_years/nonradiation_tau_years]. Parameters are not inferred from microbes or actual rock dose-depth profiles. There is no ejection-shock, atmospheric-entry, ocean-delivery or ecological-establishment term.'),('Independent validation still required','(1) Reconstruct original geophysical and water-budget time series and reproduce every manuscript figure from source data. (2) Integrate early Solar System N-body dynamics with planetary migration, impactor populations, realistic ejecta speeds, long integration windows, Jovian satellites and encounter-to-impact mapping. (3) Fit survival and shielding models to measured organism and rock-radiation experiments and couple launch, transit, entry and establishment. (4) Propagate joint parameter uncertainty and compare competing origins hypotheses with explicit likelihoods. These tasks cannot be claimed complete from this simplified simulation.'),('Scientific conclusion','This experiment establishes a reproducible computational baseline and parameter sensitivities, but neither verifies early Jovian-to-Venus panspermia nor determines which planet hosted life first.')]
rows=[['Route','N','<0.01 AU','95% count interval']]
for r in short.itertuples():rows.append([f'{r.source} to {r.destination}',str(r.n),f'{r.encounter_fraction:.3%}',f'{r.CI95_low:.3%} to {r.CI95_high:.3%}'])
styles=getSampleStyleSheet();pdf=new/'Validation_Report.pdf';story=[Paragraph('Orbital and Biological Sensitivity Extension',styles['Title']),Paragraph('Jovian–Venus project 021126 | 2 November 2026',styles['Normal']),Spacer(1,14)]
for title,body in sections[:2]:story.extend([Paragraph(title,styles['Heading2']),Paragraph(body,styles['BodyText']),Spacer(1,10)])
t=Table(rows,colWidths=[170,45,85,150],repeatRows=1);t.setStyle(TableStyle([('BACKGROUND',(0,0),(-1,0),colors.HexColor('#e6edf5')),('GRID',(0,0),(-1,-1),.3,colors.grey),('FONTSIZE',(0,0),(-1,-1),8),('BOTTOMPADDING',(0,0),(-1,-1),7)]));story.append(t);story.append(Spacer(1,12))
for title,body in sections[2:]:story.extend([Paragraph(title,styles['Heading2']),Paragraph(body,styles['BodyText']),Spacer(1,10)])
story.append(PageBreak())
for img in ['proxy_encounters.png','survival_sensitivity.png']:
 story.append(Image(str(P/'Figures'/img),width=6.4*inch,height=3.55*inch));story.append(Spacer(1,15))
SimpleDocTemplate(str(pdf),pagesize=(612,792),rightMargin=40,leftMargin=40,topMargin=40,bottomMargin=40).build(story)
doc=Document();doc.add_heading('Orbital and Biological Sensitivity Extension',0);doc.add_paragraph('Jovian–Venus project 021126 | 2 November 2026')
for title,body in sections[:2]:doc.add_heading(title,2);doc.add_paragraph(body)
t=doc.add_table(rows=0, cols=4);t.style='Light Shading Accent 1'
for rr in rows:
 c=t.add_row().cells
 for i,v in enumerate(rr):c[i].text=v
for title,body in sections[2:]:doc.add_heading(title,2);doc.add_paragraph(body)
for img in ['proxy_encounters.png','survival_sensitivity.png']:doc.add_picture(str(P/'Figures'/img),width=Inches(6.1))
doc.save(new/'Validation_Report.docx')
(new/'README.md').write_text('VALIDATION EXTENSION — reproducible but NOT independently validated historical panspermia research.\n\nRun: python run_validation.py\nRequires numpy pandas scipy matplotlib. Random seed 20261102.\nSource: original project materials retained in accompanying archive.\nCaution: no real N-body Jovian satellite transfers; no experimentally calibrated microbial survival.\n')
zipname=Path('/mnt/data/Jovian_Venus_Study_021126_Validation_Extended.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(zipname,'w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
 for f in out.rglob('*'):

"""Re-run numerical audits and conditional Jovian threshold sensitivity. Requires numpy, matplotlib."""
from pathlib import Path
import csv, math
import numpy as np
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
base=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]; rows=list(csv.DictReader(open(base/'Data/reference_endpoints.csv')))
radii={'Earth':6371e3,'Mars':3389.5e3,'Venus wet':6051.8e3,'Venus hot':6051.8e3}
for r in rows:
 h=float(r['habitable_gel_m']);u=float(r['unbound_gel_m']);assert 0<=h<=u
 implied=4*math.pi*radii[r['branch']]**2*h/1e16
 print(r['branch'],r['age_ga'],'reported',r['suitable_1e16_m3'],'implied from GEL',round(implied,3))
for name,t in {'Europa':5.64e7,'Ganymede':5.08e7,'Callisto':2.93e8}.items():
 p=np.logspace(-12,-2,121);plt.loglog(p,t/p,label=name)
plt.xlabel('Return probability');plt.ylabel('Required amplification B');plt.legend();plt.tight_layout();plt.savefig(base/'Figures/jovian_return_thresholds_reproduced.png',dpi=180)

"""Reproduce plots and checks from the published manuscript's stated reference endpoints.
These are manuscript-transcribed scenario outputs, NOT independently regenerated physics.
Run: python Code/reproduce_endpoints.py from project root.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
root=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
rows=list(csv.DictReader((root/'Data/reference_endpoints.csv').open()))
fig, ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(8,5))
for planet in ['Earth','Mars','Venus wet','Venus hot']:
    sub=[r for r in rows if r['branch']==planet]
    ax.plot([float(r['age_ga']) for r in sub],[float(r['habitable_gel_m']) for r in sub],marker='o',label=planet)
ax.set_xlabel('Age (Ga before present)')
ax.set_ylabel('Modeled 0–80 °C suitable-equivalent water (m GEL)')
ax.set_title('Manuscript endpoint comparison (not full time series)')
ax.invert_xaxis();ax.legend();fig.tight_layout()
fig.savefig(root/'Figures/endpoint_comparison.png',dpi=220)
plt.close(fig)
print('Saved endpoint comparison; intermediate ages and full model not reconstructed.')


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/crater_analysis/mars_fresh_only_inversion.py
================================================================================
"""FRESH-ONLY Bayesian inversion: atmospheric pressure, gas mixture, impactor class.
Uses only Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh-crater elevation-bin means.
No degraded craters, erosion, valley networks, or climate-model priors enter likelihood.
NO independent meteorite classification is possible from these data.
No fragmentation/ablation hydrocode: strength is used only as a survival filter.
Run: python mars_impactor_class_joint.py
"""
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.special import logsumexp
from scipy.stats import qmc,norm
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
raw=pd.read_csv(ROOT/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')
classes=['2-3','3-4','4-5']; mids=np.array([2.5,3.5,4.5]); z=np.sort(raw.elevation_km.unique())
def matrix(col):
 return np.array([raw[raw.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)].set_index('elevation_km').reindex(z)[col].to_numpy(float) for c in classes])
fresh=matrix('fresh_depth_km')
fsd=matrix('fresh_sd_km')
SF=np.sqrt((fsd/np.sqrt(20))**2+.02**2)
pressures=[.5,1.,1.5,2.,3.]; h2s=[0.,.01,.03,.05,.07,.10]; rhs=[.35,.75]
# Candidate physical *analogs*, not meteorite spectral classifications.
meteorites={
 'Carbonaceous chondrite':(1800,900,0.8),
 'Ordinary chondrite':(3400,5e6,1.0),
 'Stony-iron':(4700,2e7,1.0),
 'Iron':(7800,1e8,1.0)}
# strength [Pa] are broad nominal survival thresholds, not measured Noachian materials.
N=64; u=np.clip(qmc.Sobol(d=10,scramble=True,seed=3852023).random_base2(6),1e-9,1-1e-9)
g=3.71; T=np.clip(270+12*norm.ppf(u[:,0]),235,300)
angle=np.arcsin(np.sqrt(.08+.9*u[:,1])); density_factor=np.exp(.18*norm.ppf(u[:,2]))
relief_ratio=np.exp(np.log(.105)+.10*norm.ppf(u[:,3])); diameter_scale=np.exp(.12*norm.ppf(u[:,4]))
# Size distribution: differential dN/dD~D^-3.5; integrated logarithmic bins ~D^-2.5.
D=np.geomspace(35,1400,75)[None,:,None]
V=np.array([7000.,10000.,13000.,17000.,22000.])[None,None,:]
weights=(D/100)**(-2.5)*np.array([.12,.25,.29,.22,.12])[None,None,:]
weights=np.broadcast_to(weights,(1,75,5)); weights=weights/weights.sum()
def vapor_pressure(t):
 c=t-273.15
 return 611.21*np.exp((18.678-c/234.5)*(c/(257.14+c)))
records=[]; per_bin=[]
for p in pressures:
 for h in h2s:
  for rh in rhs:
   water=np.minimum(.015,rh*vapor_pressure(T)/(p*1e5)); co2=1-h-water
   R=co2*188.9+h*4124+water*461.5; H=R*T/g
   for name,(density,strength,cd) in meteorites.items():
    initial=np.full((N,3,len(z)),np.nan); survival=np.zeros((N,3,len(z)))
    rho_i=density*density_factor
    for k,alt in enumerate(z):
     column=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/g
     beta=(2/3)*rho_i[:,None,None]*D/cd
     v=V*np.exp(-column[:,None,None]/(2*beta*np.sin(angle[:,None,None])))
     # Approximate fragmentation flag: dynamic pressure at max atmospheric density
     # is NOT a validated breakup model. Reduced weight, rather than hard exclusion.
     rho_air=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/(R*T)
     q=.5*rho_air[:,None,None]*v*v
     survive=np.exp(-np.maximum(q/strength-1,0)*.7)
     crater=1.3*1.16*diameter_scale[:,None,None]*(rho_i[:,None,None]/3000)**(1/3)*D**.78*(v*v/g)**.22/1000
     for j,(lo,hi) in enumerate([(2,3),(3,4),(4,5)]):
      ww=weights*survive*((crater>=lo)&(crater<hi))
      normw=ww.sum(axis=(1,2))
      dmean=np.divide((ww*crater).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      vmean=np.divide((ww*v).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      initial[:,j,k]=relief_ratio*dmean*(vmean/12000)**.15
      survival[:,j,k]=normw
    residual_f=((initial-fresh[None,:,:])/SF[None,:,:])**2
    lf=-.5*np.sum(residual_f,axis=(1,2))
    lf[~np.isfinite(initial).all(axis=(1,2))]=-np.inf
    evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)
    records.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,h2o_pct=100*water.mean(),co2_pct=100*co2.mean(),
       relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,log_evidence=evidence))
    for j,crater_bin in enumerate(classes):
      lfj=-.5*np.sum(residual_f[:,j,:],axis=1)
      lfj[~np.isfinite(initial[:,j,:]).all(axis=1)]=-np.inf
      per_bin.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,
          crater_bin_km=crater_bin,log_evidence=logsumexp(lfj)-np.log(N)))
res=pd.DataFrame(records); res['posterior']=np.exp(res.log_evidence-logsumexp(res.log_evidence))
res.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_grid.csv',index=False)
summary=[]
for col in ['pressure_bar','meteorite_class','h2_pct','relative_humidity']:
 tab=res.groupby(col,as_index=False).posterior.sum().rename(columns={'posterior':'probability'})
 tab.to_csv(ROOT/f'mars_fresh_only_posterior_{col}.csv',index=False)
 summary.append((col,tab))
binres=pd.DataFrame(per_bin)
for crater_bin,part in binres.groupby('crater_bin_km'):
 part=part.copy();part['posterior']=np.exp(part.log_evidence-logsumexp(part.log_evidence))
 binres.loc[part.index,'within_bin_posterior']=part.posterior
bybin=binres.groupby(['crater_bin_km','meteorite_class'],as_index=False).within_bin_posterior.sum()
bybin.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_class_by_crater_bin.csv',index=False)
# PCA includes pressure, impactor density, and gas composition; gases sum to one.
X=res[['pressure_bar','co2_pct','h2_pct','h2o_pct']].copy()
X['impactor_density_kg_m3']=res.meteorite_class.map({k:v[0] for k,v in meteorites.items()})
scaler=StandardScaler();scaled=scaler.fit_transform(X)
pca=PCA(n_components=4).fit(scaled)
load=pd.DataFrame(pca.components_.T,index=X.columns,columns=[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)])
load.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_loadings.csv')
pd.DataFrame({'component':[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)],'variance_fraction':pca.explained_variance_ratio_}).to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_variance.csv',index=False)
fig,axs=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(12,9),layout='constrained')
pr=dict(summary)['pressure_bar'];axs[0,0].bar(pr.pressure_bar.astype(str),pr.probability,color='#477eac');axs[0,0].set(title='Pressure posterior',xlabel='bar',ylabel='Probability')
cl=dict(summary)['meteorite_class'];axs[0,1].barh(cl.meteorite_class,cl.probability,color='#788d79');axs[0,1].set(title='Impactor-class posterior',xlabel='Probability')
pivot=bybin.pivot(index='crater_bin_km',columns='meteorite_class',values='within_bin_posterior').loc[classes]
pivot.plot(kind='bar',ax=axs[1,0]);axs[1,0].set(title='Class ranking by final crater diameter',xlabel='Final crater diameter (km)',ylabel='Within-bin probability');axs[1,0].legend(fontsize=7)
axs[1,1].bar(np.arange(4)+1,pca.explained_variance_ratio_,color='#7c87a6');axs[1,1].set(title='PCA of pressure, gases and impactor density',xlabel='Principal component',ylabel='Variance explained')
fig.suptitle('Fresh-crater-only inversion: atmospheric pressure and impactor classes\nClass priors and simplified survival law strongly affect rankings',fontsize=12)
fig.savefig(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_figure.png',dpi=180,bbox_inches='tight');plt.close(fig)
with open(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_methodology.txt','w') as f:
 f.write('EXPLORATORY MODEL, NOT METEORITE IDENTIFICATION\n\n')
 f.write('Uses ONLY Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh crater relief elevation-bin means.\n')
 f.write('NO degraded crater data, NO valley networks, NO climate/erosion models enter the likelihood.\n')
 f.write('Equal class, pressure, H2 and humidity grid priors.\n')
 f.write('Crater gravity-regime scaling and impactor survival are simplified; ablation, fragmentation,\n')
 f.write('impactor size-frequency distributions and rock strengths are poorly constrained.\n')
 f.write('Likelihood assumes effective crater count 20 and uncertainty floors 20/30 meters.\n')
 f.write('The highest posterior class is conditional on these assumptions and cannot identify a meteorite class.\n')
 f.write('PCA is descriptive, not causal. Gas fractions sum to 100%, creating dependence.\n')
 f.write('The paper has 2-3, 3-4, 4-5 km bins; no 1-2 km data are present.\n')
print('PRESSURE',dict(summary)['pressure_bar'].to_string(index=False))
print('CLASS',cl.to_string(index=False))
print('BY BIN',bybin.to_string(index=False))
print('PCA',pca.explained_variance_ratio_)


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/crater_analysis/reproduce_fresh_fits.py
================================================================================
"""FRESH-ONLY Bayesian inversion: atmospheric pressure, gas mixture, impactor class.
Uses only Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh-crater elevation-bin means.
No degraded craters, erosion, valley networks, or climate-model priors enter likelihood.
NO independent meteorite classification is possible from these data.
No fragmentation/ablation hydrocode: strength is used only as a survival filter.
Run: python mars_impactor_class_joint.py
"""
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.special import logsumexp
from scipy.stats import qmc,norm
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
raw=pd.read_csv(ROOT/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')
classes=['2-3','3-4','4-5']; mids=np.array([2.5,3.5,4.5]); z=np.sort(raw.elevation_km.unique())
def matrix(col):
 return np.array([raw[raw.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)].set_index('elevation_km').reindex(z)[col].to_numpy(float) for c in classes])
fresh=matrix('fresh_depth_km')
fsd=matrix('fresh_sd_km')
SF=np.sqrt((fsd/np.sqrt(20))**2+.02**2)
pressures=[.5,1.,1.5,2.,3.]; h2s=[0.,.01,.03,.05,.07,.10]; rhs=[.35,.75]
# Candidate physical *analogs*, not meteorite spectral classifications.
meteorites={
 'Carbonaceous chondrite':(1800,900,0.8),
 'Ordinary chondrite':(3400,5e6,1.0),
 'Stony-iron':(4700,2e7,1.0),
 'Iron':(7800,1e8,1.0)}
# strength [Pa] are broad nominal survival thresholds, not measured Noachian materials.
N=64; u=np.clip(qmc.Sobol(d=10,scramble=True,seed=3852023).random_base2(6),1e-9,1-1e-9)
g=3.71; T=np.clip(270+12*norm.ppf(u[:,0]),235,300)
angle=np.arcsin(np.sqrt(.08+.9*u[:,1])); density_factor=np.exp(.18*norm.ppf(u[:,2]))
relief_ratio=np.exp(np.log(.105)+.10*norm.ppf(u[:,3])); diameter_scale=np.exp(.12*norm.ppf(u[:,4]))
# Size distribution: differential dN/dD~D^-3.5; integrated logarithmic bins ~D^-2.5.
D=np.geomspace(35,1400,75)[None,:,None]
V=np.array([7000.,10000.,13000.,17000.,22000.])[None,None,:]
weights=(D/100)**(-2.5)*np.array([.12,.25,.29,.22,.12])[None,None,:]
weights=np.broadcast_to(weights,(1,75,5)); weights=weights/weights.sum()
def vapor_pressure(t):
 c=t-273.15
 return 611.21*np.exp((18.678-c/234.5)*(c/(257.14+c)))
records=[]; per_bin=[]; best_by_pressure={}
for p in pressures:
 for h in h2s:
  for rh in rhs:
   water=np.minimum(.015,rh*vapor_pressure(T)/(p*1e5)); co2=1-h-water
   R=co2*188.9+h*4124+water*461.5; H=R*T/g
   for name,(density,strength,cd) in meteorites.items():
    initial=np.full((N,3,len(z)),np.nan); survival=np.zeros((N,3,len(z)))
    rho_i=density*density_factor
    for k,alt in enumerate(z):
     column=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/g
     beta=(2/3)*rho_i[:,None,None]*D/cd
     v=V*np.exp(-column[:,None,None]/(2*beta*np.sin(angle[:,None,None])))
     # Approximate fragmentation flag: dynamic pressure at max atmospheric density
     # is NOT a validated breakup model. Reduced weight, rather than hard exclusion.
     rho_air=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/(R*T)
     q=.5*rho_air[:,None,None]*v*v
     survive=np.exp(-np.maximum(q/strength-1,0)*.7)
     crater=1.3*1.16*diameter_scale[:,None,None]*(rho_i[:,None,None]/3000)**(1/3)*D**.78*(v*v/g)**.22/1000
     for j,(lo,hi) in enumerate([(2,3),(3,4),(4,5)]):
      ww=weights*survive*((crater>=lo)&(crater<hi))
      normw=ww.sum(axis=(1,2))
      dmean=np.divide((ww*crater).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      vmean=np.divide((ww*v).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      initial[:,j,k]=relief_ratio*dmean*(vmean/12000)**.15
      survival[:,j,k]=normw
    residual_f=((initial-fresh[None,:,:])/SF[None,:,:])**2
    lf=-.5*np.sum(residual_f,axis=(1,2))
    lf[~np.isfinite(initial).all(axis=(1,2))]=-np.inf
    evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)
    ix=int(np.argmax(lf))
    if np.isfinite(lf[ix]) and (p not in best_by_pressure or lf[ix]>best_by_pressure[p]['ll']):
     best_by_pressure[p]={'ll':float(lf[ix]),'prediction':initial[ix].copy(),'h2':h,'rh':rh,'class':name}
    records.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,h2o_pct=100*water.mean(),co2_pct=100*co2.mean(),
       relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,log_evidence=evidence))
    for j,crater_bin in enumerate(classes):
      lfj=-.5*np.sum(residual_f[:,j,:],axis=1)
      lfj[~np.isfinite(initial[:,j,:]).all(axis=1)]=-np.inf
      per_bin.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,
          crater_bin_km=crater_bin,log_evidence=logsumexp(lfj)-np.log(N)))
res=pd.DataFrame(records); res['posterior']=np.exp(res.log_evidence-logsumexp(res.log_evidence))
res.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_grid.csv',index=False)
summary=[]
for col in ['pressure_bar','meteorite_class','h2_pct','relative_humidity']:
 tab=res.groupby(col,as_index=False).posterior.sum().rename(columns={'posterior':'probability'})
 tab.to_csv(ROOT/f'mars_fresh_only_posterior_{col}.csv',index=False)
 summary.append((col,tab))
binres=pd.DataFrame(per_bin)
for crater_bin,part in binres.groupby('crater_bin_km'):
 part=part.copy();part['posterior']=np.exp(part.log_evidence-logsumexp(part.log_evidence))
 binres.loc[part.index,'within_bin_posterior']=part.posterior
bybin=binres.groupby(['crater_bin_km','meteorite_class'],as_index=False).within_bin_posterior.sum()
bybin.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_class_by_crater_bin.csv',index=False)
# PCA includes pressure, impactor density, and gas composition; gases sum to one.
X=res[['pressure_bar','co2_pct','h2_pct','h2o_pct']].copy()
X['impactor_density_kg_m3']=res.meteorite_class.map({k:v[0] for k,v in meteorites.items()})
scaler=StandardScaler();scaled=scaler.fit_transform(X)
pca=PCA(n_components=4).fit(scaled)
load=pd.DataFrame(pca.components_.T,index=X.columns,columns=[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)])
load.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_loadings.csv')
pd.DataFrame({'component':[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)],'variance_fraction':pca.explained_variance_ratio_}).to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_variance.csv',index=False)
fig,axs=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(12,9),layout='constrained')
pr=dict(summary)['pressure_bar'];axs[0,0].bar(pr.pressure_bar.astype(str),pr.probability,color='#477eac');axs[0,0].set(title='Pressure posterior',xlabel='bar',ylabel='Probability')
cl=dict(summary)['meteorite_class'];axs[0,1].barh(cl.meteorite_class,cl.probability,color='#788d79');axs[0,1].set(title='Impactor-class posterior',xlabel='Probability')
pivot=bybin.pivot(index='crater_bin_km',columns='meteorite_class',values='within_bin_posterior').loc[classes]
pivot.plot(kind='bar',ax=axs[1,0]);axs[1,0].set(title='Class ranking by final crater diameter',xlabel='Final crater diameter (km)',ylabel='Within-bin probability');axs[1,0].legend(fontsize=7)
axs[1,1].bar(np.arange(4)+1,pca.explained_variance_ratio_,color='#7c87a6');axs[1,1].set(title='PCA of pressure, gases and impactor density',xlabel='Principal component',ylabel='Variance explained')
fig.suptitle('Fresh-crater-only inversion: atmospheric pressure and impactor classes\nClass priors and simplified survival law strongly affect rankings',fontsize=12)
fig.savefig(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_figure.png',dpi=180,bbox_inches='tight');plt.close(fig)
with open(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_methodology.txt','w') as f:
 f.write('EXPLORATORY MODEL, NOT METEORITE IDENTIFICATION\n\n')
 f.write('Uses ONLY Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh crater relief elevation-bin means.\n')
 f.write('NO degraded crater data, NO valley networks, NO climate/erosion models enter the likelihood.\n')
 f.write('Equal class, pressure, H2 and humidity grid priors.\n')
 f.write('Crater gravity-regime scaling and impactor survival are simplified; ablation, fragmentation,\n')
 f.write('impactor size-frequency distributions and rock strengths are poorly constrained.\n')
 f.write('Likelihood assumes effective crater count 20 and uncertainty floors 20/30 meters.\n')
 f.write('The highest posterior class is conditional on these assumptions and cannot identify a meteorite class.\n')
 f.write('PCA is descriptive, not causal. Gas fractions sum to 100%, creating dependence.\n')
 f.write('The paper has 2-3, 3-4, 4-5 km bins; no 1-2 km data are present.\n')
print('PRESSURE',dict(summary)['pressure_bar'].to_string(index=False))
print('CLASS',cl.to_string(index=False))
print('BY BIN',bybin.to_string(index=False))
print('PCA',pca.explained_variance_ratio_)

fitrows=[]
for p,b in best_by_pressure.items():
 for j,c in enumerate(classes):
  for k,alt in enumerate(z):fitrows.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,diameter_bin_km=c,elevation_km=alt,observed_depth_km=fresh[j,k],sigma_km=SF[j,k],predicted_depth_km=b['prediction'][j,k],h2_pct=100*b['h2'],relative_humidity=b['rh'],impactor_analog=b['class'],chi2=-2*b['ll']))
pd.DataFrame(fitrows).to_csv(ROOT/'fresh_best_fit_by_pressure.csv',index=False)


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/code/derive_water_and_radiation.py
================================================================================
"""Reproducible extension of the archived 4.4 Ga conditional aqueous model.
Run from the extracted bundle root: python code/derive_water_and_radiation.py.
All volumes are scenario predictions, not water detections. Dose thresholds
are sensitivity definitions, NOT mineral conversion or microbial survival laws.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,csv,json,math
import numpy as np
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
OLD=P/'source_model'
if not OLD.exists(): OLD=P.parents[0]/'archive/Mars_Earth_Habitability_44Ga_Nursery_Project'
sys.path.insert(0,str(OLD/'source'))
import build_model as bm
import thermal_foundation as th
Z=bm.Z; DZ=bm.DZ; AGES=bm.AGES
AREAS={'Mars':144.37e6,'Earth':510.1e6} # km²; exact project reference areas
GRAVITY={'Mars':3.71,'Earth':9.81}
SECONDS_YEAR=365.25*86400
ROUTES=[dict(source='Mars',target='Earth',count=1048,total=40500,median_Myr=5.2),
        dict(source='Mars',target='Venus',count=617,total=40500,median_Myr=5.8),
        dict(source='Earth',target='Mars',count=79,total=43500,median_Myr=4.7),
        dict(source='Earth',target='Venus',count=5713,total=43500,median_Myr=1.7)]
AU={'Mars':1.524,'Earth':1.0,'Venus':.723}
MATERIALS={'hydrated_basalt':dict(density_kg_m3=2800,attenuation_m=1e-6),
 'fresh_basalt':dict(density_kg_m3=3000,attenuation_m=.3e-6),
 'ultramafic_endmember':dict(density_kg_m3=3300,attenuation_m=.1e-6)}
PARAMS=dict(XUV_flux_today_1AU_W_m2=.00464,XUV_band_nm=[.1,120],
 XUV_evolution_exponent=1.23,solar_age_today_Gyr=4.567,
 GCR_reference_Gy_yr=.2,GCR_attenuation_kg_m2=1500.,
 reference_dose_threshold_Gy=100000.,sphere_radii_m=[.01,.1,1.,5.,10.],
 ejected_source_volume_per_10Myr_km3=1.,composition_scenarios={
 'fresh_dominated':[.05,.85,.10],'balanced_illustration':[.20,.70,.10],
 'hydration_rich':[.50,.40,.10]},
 composition_interpretation='Assumed volume fractions hydrated basalt, fresh basalt, ultramafic endmember. No inferred empirical percentages; ordinary-chondritic admixture is unmeasured.',
 radiation_interpretation='Radial exponential attenuation surrogate. Doses are absorbed-energy scenarios; fractions exceeding D* are not percent chemical conversion or survival. No particle transport, secondary cascades, SEP history, ablation, rotation trajectory integration, or reaction kinetics.')

def write(name,rows):
 rows=list(rows);(P/'data').mkdir(exist_ok=True)
 with (P/'data'/name).open('w',newline='') as f:
  w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

def quantile_depth(w,q):
 return float(np.interp(q,np.cumsum(w)/np.sum(w),Z)) if np.sum(w)>0 else None

def phase_mask(T,pl):
 """Pure-water hydrostatic phase-screen scenario below the critical point.
 A liquid phase is allowed at 0 to 373.946 C if pore pressure exceeds saturation.
 Ice pressure shifts, brines, supercritical water and connectedness are omitted.
 The archived 0-80 C metric is exported separately and unchanged.
 """
 tk=np.clip(T+273.15,273.15,647.096)
 tt=1-tk/647.096
 a=[-7.85951783,1.84408259,-11.7866497,22.6807411,-15.9618719,1.80122502]
 powers=[1,1.5,3,3.5,4,7.5]
 psat=22.064e6*np.exp(647.096/tk*sum(aa*tt**pp for aa,pp in zip(a,powers)))
 pore_pressure=(600 if pl=='Mars' else 101325)+1000*GRAVITY[pl]*Z*1000
 return (T>=0)&(T<373.946)&(pore_pressure>=psat)

def dose_values(age,route,mat,radius,threshold=1e5):
 """Surface-averaged XUV; radial GCR surrogate, integrated during transit.
 Solar aging is integrated analytically over the median transit, ending no later
 than the present. A launch today has future exposure using the same power law.
 """
 rho=mat['density_kg_m3'];delta=mat['attenuation_m'];travel=route['median_Myr']*1e6
 solar0=PARAMS['solar_age_today_Gyr']-age
 solar1=solar0+travel/1e9;p=PARAMS['XUV_evolution_exponent']
 agefactor=PARAMS['solar_age_today_Gyr']**p*(solar1**(1-p)-solar0**(1-p))/((1-p)*(solar1-solar0))
 aeff=(AU[route['source']]+AU[route['target']])/2
 flux=PARAMS['XUV_flux_today_1AU_W_m2']*agefactor/aeff**2
 uv0=flux*travel*SECONDS_YEAR/(4*rho*delta)
 cr0=PARAMS['GCR_reference_Gy_yr']*travel
 def dose(d):return uv0*math.exp(-d/delta)+cr0*math.exp(-rho*d/PARAMS['GCR_attenuation_kg_m2'])
 def depth_above(which):
  if which=='uv':return float(np.clip(delta*math.log(uv0/threshold),0,radius)) if uv0>threshold else 0.
  if dose(radius)>=threshold:return radius
  if dose(0)<threshold:return 0.
  lo=0.;hi=radius
  for _ in range(80):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   if dose(mid)>=threshold:lo=mid
   else:hi=mid
  return (lo+hi)/2
 dep=depth_above('all');uvdep=depth_above('uv')
 return dict(mean_XUV_flux_W_m2=flux,XUV_surface_absorbed_dose_Gy=uv0,
  GCR_surface_dose_Gy=cr0,central_total_dose_Gy=dose(radius),
  threshold_Gy=threshold,threshold_shell_depth_m=dep,
  volume_above_threshold_fraction=1-(1-dep/radius)**3,
  XUV_only_threshold_shell_depth_m=uvdep,
  XUV_only_volume_above_threshold_fraction=1-(1-uvdep/radius)**3,
  inferred_chemical_conversion_percent='',chemical_conversion_status='not identifiable without substrate-specific reaction kinetics')

def run():
 rows=[];layers=[];small={};checks=[]
 for scenario,s in bm.SC.items():
  small[scenario]={'Mars':[],'Earth':[],'budgets':[]}
  for age in AGES:
   budget=bm.budget(age,s);small[scenario]['budgets'].append(dict(age_Ga=float(age),**budget))
   for pl in ['Mars','Earth']:
    planet=bm.planet(pl,age,s);area=AREAS[pl]
    water=np.zeros_like(Z);eligible=np.zeros_like(Z);H=np.zeros_like(Z);phase=np.zeros_like(Z)
    t_eligible=np.zeros_like(Z);tH=np.zeros_like(Z);t_phase=np.zeros_like(Z)
    sunband=dict(water=0.,eligible=0.,H=0.,phase=0.)
    closures={}
    for domain,col in planet['columns']:
     dv=col['cell_water'];T=col['temp'];mask=(T>=0)&(T<=80);pm=phase_mask(T,pl)
     hh=dv*col['score'];water+=dv;eligible+=dv*mask;phase+=dv*pm;H+=hh
     t_eligible+=dv*mask*T;tH+=hh*T;t_phase+=dv*pm*T
     closures[domain]=col['closure']
     band=(Z>=5.4)&(Z<8.0)
     sunband['water']+=float(sum(dv[band]));sunband['eligible']+=float(sum((dv*mask)[band]))
     sunband['H']+=float(sum(hh[band]));sunband['phase']+=float(sum((dv*pm)[band]))
     for j,z in enumerate(Z):
      layers.append(dict(scenario=scenario,planet=pl,domain=domain,age_Ga=float(age),depth_km=float(z),
       temperature_C=float(T[j]),assigned_free_pore_water_m3=float(dv[j]*1e9),
       potential_liquid_phase_water_m3=float(dv[j]*pm[j]*1e9),
       liquid_0_80C_water_m3=float(dv[j]*mask[j]*1e9),
       liquid_0_80C_GEL_m=float(dv[j]*mask[j]/area*1000),
       suitable_water_equivalent_m3=float(hh[j]*1e9),
       effective_porosity=float(col['phi'][j]),thermal_score=float(col['score'][j])))
    W=float(sum(water));L=float(sum(eligible));Vphase=float(sum(phase));h=float(sum(H))
    peak=int(np.argmax(H));support=np.where(eligible>0)[0]
    row=dict(scenario=scenario,planet=pl,age_Ga=float(age),
     total_unbound_inventory_GEL_m=budget['free'] if pl=='Mars' else '',
     assigned_free_pore_water_m3=W*1e9,assigned_free_pore_water_GEL_m=W/area*1000,
     potential_liquid_phase_water_m3=Vphase*1e9,potential_liquid_phase_GEL_m=Vphase/area*1000,
     liquid_0_80C_water_m3=L*1e9,liquid_0_80C_GEL_m=L/area*1000,
     suitable_water_equivalent_m3=h*1e9,suitable_water_equivalent_GEL_m=h/area*1000,
     normalized_suitability_free_pore_water=h/W,normalized_suitability_phase_liquid=h/Vphase if Vphase else 0,
     normalized_suitability_0_80C_liquid=h/L if L else 0,
     liquid_0_80C_mean_temperature_C=float(sum(t_eligible)/L) if L else None,
     phase_liquid_mean_temperature_C=float(sum(t_phase)/Vphase) if Vphase else None,
     suitability_weighted_mean_depth_km=float(sum(H*Z)/h),
     suitability_weighted_mean_temperature_C=float(sum(tH)/h),
     peak_habitat_depth_km=float(Z[peak]),peak_habitat_temperature_C=float(tH[peak]/H[peak]),
     lens_top_km=float(Z[support[0]]-DZ/2),lens_base_km=float(Z[support[-1]]+DZ/2),
     lens_10pct_depth_km=quantile_depth(eligible,.10),lens_90pct_depth_km=quantile_depth(eligible,.90),
     operational_pore_closure_km=closures.get('Mars',None),
     prescribed_Mars_closure_cap_km=s['mars_close'] if pl=='Mars' else '',
     Earth_continental_closure_km=closures.get('Earth_continent',None),
     Earth_oceanic_closure_km=closures.get('Earth_ocean',None),
     water_5p4_8km_GEL_m=sunband['water']/area*1000,
     liquid_0_80C_5p4_8km_GEL_m=sunband['eligible']/area*1000,
     phase_liquid_5p4_8km_GEL_m=sunband['phase']/area*1000)
    assert np.isclose(h,planet['volume'],rtol=1e-12)
    assert np.isclose(L,planet['liquid'],rtol=1e-12)
    assert 0<=h<=L+1e-5<=Vphase+1e-5<=W+1e-5
    rows.append(row)
    view={k:v for k,v in row.items() if k not in ['scenario','planet']}
    view['liquid_temperature_profile_C']=[round(float(tt/ww),5) if ww else None for tt,ww in zip(t_eligible[::5],eligible[::5])]
    view.update(depths_km=Z[::5].tolist(),eligible_GEL_per_km=(eligible/(area*DZ)*1000)[::5].tolist(),
      suitability_GEL_per_km=(H/(area*DZ)*1000)[::5].tolist())
    small[scenario][pl].append(view)
 write('time_horizon_metrics.csv',rows);write('depth_time_water_temperature.csv',layers)
 peaks={}
 for sc in bm.SC:
  peaks[sc]={}
  for pl in ['Mars','Earth']:
   rr=[r for r in rows if r['scenario']==sc and r['planet']==pl];peaks[sc][pl]={}
   for key in ['suitable_water_equivalent_m3','normalized_suitability_free_pore_water','normalized_suitability_phase_liquid','normalized_suitability_0_80C_liquid']:
    high=max(r[key] for r in rr);pp=[r['age_Ga'] for r in rr if r[key]>=.9*high]
    peaks[sc][pl][key]=dict(maximum=high,peak_age_Ga=max(rr,key=lambda r:r[key])['age_Ga'],
     ages_at_least_90pct_maximum_Ga=pp)
 write('snapshots_3p8Ga.csv',[r for r in rows if r['age_Ga']==3.8])
 rad=[]
 for age in AGES:
  for route in ROUTES:
   for mat,props in MATERIALS.items():
    for radius in PARAMS['sphere_radii_m']:
     d=dose_values(float(age),route,props,radius)
     assert 0<=d['volume_above_threshold_fraction']<=1
     rad.append(dict(age_Ga=float(age),source=route['source'],target=route['target'],
      transit_Myr=route['median_Myr'],material=mat,sphere_radius_m=radius,**d))
 write('ejecta_dose_sphere_timeseries.csv',rad)
 exchange=[]
 for r in ROUTES:
  p=r['count']/r['total'];v=p*PARAMS['ejected_source_volume_per_10Myr_km3']
  exchange.append(dict(**r,particle_transfer_fraction=p,
   assumed_source_volume_km3=1.,conditional_delivered_volume_m3=v*1e9,
   mean_delivered_rate_over_10Myr_m3_yr=v*1e9/1e7,
   interpretation='Equal-volume-particle transfer assumption; normalization only; not measured absolute flux'))
 write('exchange_normalized_volumes_rates.csv',exchange)
 write('exchange_normalized_timeseries.csv',[dict(launch_age_Ga=float(age),**r,epoch_interpretation='Constant transfer ensemble reused as a conditional normalization, not reconstructed ancient dynamics') for age in AGES for r in exchange])
 sensitivity=[]
 for star in [1e4,1e5,1e6]:
  for radius in [.1,1,5,10]:
   for mat,prop in MATERIALS.items():
    sensitivity.append(dict(age_Ga=3.8,threshold_Gy=star,radius_m=radius,material=mat,**{k:v for k,v in dose_values(3.8,ROUTES[0],prop,radius,star).items() if k!='threshold_Gy'}))
 write('radiation_threshold_sensitivity_3p8Ga.csv',sensitivity)
 data=dict(ages_Ga=AGES.tolist(),scenarios=small,peaks=peaks,parameters=PARAMS,
  materials=MATERIALS,routes=exchange,units='m GEL, m3 water, km depth, degrees C, Gy absorbed dose',
  phase_screen='Pure-water hydrostatic screen with 0–373.946 C; uncertain pore pressure. IAPWS saturation equation. Separate 0–80 C legacy metric preserved.',
  closure_definition='Deepest midpoint with post-occlusion porosity above 1e-5; cap is prescribed, not a detected boundary.')
 (P/'data/model_extension.json').write_text(json.dumps(data,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False))
 (P/'data/peak_intervals.json').write_text(json.dumps(peaks,indent=2))
 (P/'data/assumptions.json').write_text(json.dumps(PARAMS,indent=2))
 (P/'data/validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(time_horizon_rows=len(rows),depth_time_rows=len(layers),
  radiation_rows=len(rad),checks=['legacy H and 0–80 C water reproduced at every time and scenario',
  'H ≤ V liquid 0–80 ≤ V phase liquid ≤ assigned pore water','all shell volume fractions in [0,1]',
  'spherical shell fraction uses cubed radius ratio, not cross-sectional area','GEL uses separate planetary areas'],
  status='passed'),indent=2))
 print(json.dumps([r for r in rows if r['scenario']=='reference' and r['age_Ga'] in [4.4,3.8,0]],indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':run()


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/code/focused_nsr.py

"""Focused NSR alternative centred on the 2025 upper-crust water interpretation."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import build_manuscripts as b
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
d=b.new();c=b.Cites('NSR');b.front(d,b.FOCUS);d.add_page_break();d.add_heading('Abstract',1)
abstract='A proposed Martian upper-crust aquifer requires consistency among saturation, temperature and planetary water inventory. I compare a published 5.4–8 km, 520–780 m global equivalent layer interpretation with conditional crustal water histories from 4.4 billion years ago. At 3.8 billion years, the reference model retains 731.1 m unbound global equivalent layer, including 115.5 m within 0–80 °C at 34.77 °C mean temperature. Today, it retains 472 m unbound water and permits 232.0 m phase-screen liquid across all modeled depths, with only 0.916 m in the proposed upper-crust interval. The mismatch follows the imposed inventory and thermal assumptions; it does not observationally reject the seismic interpretation. Depth-resolved phase and suitability calculations identify measurements needed to distinguish a local water-bearing anomaly from a globally extensive aquifer.'
c.para(d,abstract);d.add_paragraph('Keywords: Mars; crustal water; global equivalent layer; pore closure; seismic interpretation; habitability')
d.add_heading('Introduction',1)
for t in [
 'An inferred water-bearing seismic layer must satisfy both local physical conditions and a planetary water budget. Sun et al. (2025) identify an upper-crust low-velocity interval at 5.4–8 km and interpret its pore space as liquid-water saturated, yielding a 520–780 m global equivalent layer extrapolation. That interpretation is a valuable observational target, but its global volume depends on saturation and lateral extent rather than seismic velocity alone.',
 'Other seismic and rock-physics interpretations permit deeper water or a broad range of saturation (Wright et al., 2024; Jakosky, 2025; Zha et al., 2026). Meanwhile, mineral hydration can sequester large water inventories without leaving equivalent volumes of free liquid (Scheller et al., 2021). A global water estimate must distinguish bound water, unbound water, the portion assigned to pore space and the portion permitted to remain liquid.',
 'I test that consistency using the project’s time-dependent crustal thermal and water model. This is an independent forward comparison, not a reinversion of InSight waveforms or a fitted estimate of aquifer probability. The calculation tracks global equivalent layer, water volume, depth and temperature from 4.4 Ga to the present and highlights 3.8 Ga. The comparative microbial window is evaluated separately from all potential liquid, and its suitability score is normalized to explicitly named water compartments.'
]:c.para(d,t)
b.new_results(d,c,True)
d.add_heading('Inventory constraints',1)
for t in [
 'The reference budget begins with 1,100 m GEL and adds 22 m by outgassing; retained mineral water and escape remove 550 m and 100 m, respectively. The remaining 472 m unbound inventory is an upper bound on all free water in that reference history, before assigning a location or phase. A present global aquifer of 520–780 m GEL therefore cannot fit that particular budget. The water-rich history retains 952 m and changes the test. These endmembers are jointly varied scenarios, not a posterior distribution fitted to the seismic observations.',
 'The comparison also cannot equate water lost from a surface reservoir with water demonstrably stored in a specific present layer. Mineral retention, escape and residual unbound compartments are mutually accounted components. Their histories are uncertain, and the normalization of the young-Sun escape kernel does not independently measure cumulative loss (Scheller et al., 2021; Tu et al., 2015).'
]:c.para(d,t)
d.add_heading('Temperature and pore structure',1)
for t in [
 'In the reference modern column, a −60 °C surface boundary and approximately 19.6 mW m⁻² heat flux keep much of the proposed 5.4–8 km interval below the pure-water melting point. A local heat anomaly, different conductivity, pore pressure or salinity could change that outcome. The temperature inconsistency is therefore conditional and should be resolved by direct thermal constraints rather than interpreted as a contradiction in the seismic data. The model omits advective heat transport and pressure-dependent freezing.',
 'Likewise, a prescribed maximum closure depth and a water-bearing seismic layer need not identify the same interface. Literature-informed depth caps allow an open-pore domain deeper than the proposed upper-crust layer, while viscous and mineral-occlusion tapers reduce effective porosity before the cap. Porosity, permeability and saturation are distinct quantities. Local evidence for porosity at 20–25 km cannot by itself establish a globally saturated aquifer at any depth (Knapmeyer-Endrun et al., 2025; Gyalay et al., 2020; Kilburn et al., 2022).'
]:c.para(d,t)
d.add_heading('Habitat volume and discriminating tests',1)
for t in [
 'The 3.8 Ga reference water within 0–80 °C averages 34.77 °C, whereas all phase-screen liquid averages 199.14 °C. Calling the entire liquid inventory a microbial habitat would conflate two different metrics. The H-weighted mean depth and the horizon of maximum dH/dz also differ. Both statistics are exported, alongside full-depth water and temperature profiles, so that an apparently favorable mean cannot conceal an unsuitable part of the assigned reservoir.',
 'Absolute H and H/Wₚ peak at different ages because the denominator changes with the retained water inventory. The older absolute maximum is left-censored at 4.4 Ga; the late normalized maximum is not evidence for a late origin of life. Water capacity alone establishes neither occupancy nor biological ancestry. The 0–80 °C window and tapered score are comparative definitions that omit salinity, energy flux and nutrient constraints.',
 'The strongest discriminants are regional measurements of heat flow and conductivity, seismic constraints on lateral structure, independent saturation information and improved inventories of mineral-bound water. A globally extensive 5.4–8 km aquifer must be evaluated against these constraints jointly. The supplied water-rich and conservative histories make that dependence reproducible without assigning an empirical likelihood to a selected aquifer geometry.'
]:c.para(d,t)
d.add_heading('Conclusions',1)
c.para(d,'The reference model retains 731.1 m unbound GEL at 3.8 Ga, with 115.5 m in the comparative 0–80 °C window. Its modern 472 m unbound budget and cold upper-crust profile do not reproduce the published 520–780 m GEL aquifer extrapolation. This mismatch constrains the assumptions required for agreement; it does not observationally reject a local water-bearing layer. Distinguishing inventory, phase, depth and normalization provides a testable framework for comparing seismic water interpretations with microbial habitat capacity.')
d.add_heading('Materials and methods',1)
for t in b.BRIEF.split('\n\n')[:3]:c.para(d,t)
b.append_scientific_figures(d,c,True);b.declarations(d);c.refs(d)
d.add_heading('Supplementary information',1);d.add_paragraph('NSR_Upper_Crust_GEL_Mathematical_Appendix_v2.docx supplies full native-equation methods, data tables, original figures, source-data descriptions and explicitly illustrative final landscape figures.')
path=P/'documents/NSR_Upper_Crust_GEL_Mathematical_Manuscript_v2.docx';d.save(path)
counts=json.loads((P/'data/manuscript_counts.json').read_text());counts['NSR_Upper_Crust_GEL']=dict(title_characters=len(b.FOCUS),abstract_words=len(abstract.split()),methods_summary_words=len(' '.join(b.BRIEF.split('\n\n')[:3]).split()),main_figures=4,reference_count=len(c.order),native_equations=len(d.element.xpath('.//m:oMath')),total_words_including_references_and_captions=sum(len(p.text.split()) for p in d.paragraphs))
(P/'data/manuscript_counts.json').write_text(json.dumps(counts,indent=2))
print(counts['NSR_Upper_Crust_GEL'])

f=pd.read_csv(R/'crater_analysis/fresh_best_fit_by_pressure.csv'); colors=['#8cb4cc','#538bb5','#277893','#bb793b','#b03d35']

for ax,(c,g) in zip(axs,f.groupby('diameter_bin_km',sort=False)):

 m,b=np.polyfit(o.elevation_km,o.observed_depth_km*1000,1);slopes.append({'diameter_bin_km':c,'slope_m_per_km':m,'intercept_m':b})
 for col,(p,q) in zip(colors,g.groupby('pressure_bar')):ax.plot(q.elevation_km,q.predicted_depth_km*1000,color=col,lw=1.7,label=f'{p:g} bar')
 ax.set(title=f'{c} km craters',xlabel='Elevation (km)',ylabel='Fresh-crater relief (m)');ax.grid(alpha=.15)

plt.close(fig);pd.DataFrame(slopes).to_csv(R/'crater_analysis/fresh_observed_linear_fits.csv',index=False)
pr=pd.read_csv(R/'crater_analysis/mars_fresh_only_posterior_pressure_bar.csv');fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(7,4),layout='constrained');ax.bar(pr.pressure_bar.astype(str),100*pr.probability,color=colors);ax.set(xlabel='Reference surface pressure (bar)',ylabel='Conditional grid probability (%)',title='The highest pressure tested retains most model weight');ax.text(.02,.94,'3 bar is a grid boundary, not a measured optimum',transform=ax.transAxes,fontsize=9,va='top');fig.savefig(F/'Figure_C2_Pressure_Weights.png',dpi=240);fig.savefig(F/'Figure_C2_Pressure_Weights.svg');plt.close(fig)
transition=('Fresh-crater relief provides an observational starting point for testing atmospheric boundary conditions. Archer (2023, Table 4) reports elevation-bin means for 2–3, 3–4 and 4–5 km craters. I use 24 means across eight elevation bins and retain only fresh-crater relief in the atmospheric likelihood. Fresh is a morphological category and does not independently date these impacts to 3.85 Ga. The new Figure C1 reproduces the archived entry-model grid and shows the best sampled nuisance-parameter fit at each of 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2 and 3 bar. Pressure has conditional marginal weights of 6.76%, 6.81%, 10.04%, 17.91% and 58.48%, respectively. The largest weight occurs at the upper grid boundary, so neither a uniquely preferred pressure nor an upper pressure bound is established. These fits do not independently select 1.5 bar.')
methods=('The entry surrogate integrates a size and speed distribution through an exponential atmosphere, applies nominal impactor-density and strength analogs, and converts surviving impact energy to crater relief. Equal discrete priors cover pressure, hydrogen fraction, humidity and four material analogs. Sixty-four fixed-seed Sobol samples describe nuisance parameters. The likelihood uses standard deviations divided by sqrt(20) plus a 20 m error floor; the effective sample size is assumed rather than established for each published mean. At fixed pressure, Figure C1 selects the sampled nuisance state with minimum chi-square across 24 means. Minimum chi-square values are 18.43, 17.64, 16.22, 14.31 and 11.36 in ascending pressure order. These profile fits differ from nuisance-marginalized evidence. Ablation and fragmentation are simplified and the model is not a calibrated pressure retrieval. The archived methodology mentions a 30 m floor, but the fresh-only implementation uses 20 m throughout; the code governs this reproduction. Atmospheric pressure is not fitted jointly with the subsequent pore-water model, whose imposed thermal and saturation histories remain independent scenarios.')
caption=('Figure C1. Fresh-crater relief versus elevation and best sampled entry-model predictions at each reference pressure. Points are Archer (2023, Table 4) means. Error bars show the assumed likelihood uncertainty, not reported confidence intervals: sqrt(SD²/20 + 0.02²) km. Curves select a separate nuisance state at each pressure. A better sampled fit at 3 bar does not establish a global optimum because 3 bar is the grid edge. No degraded-crater data enter this likelihood. All five curves and their residuals are supplied in crater_analysis/fresh_best_fit_by_pressure.csv.')

 t=transition.replace('Archer (2023, Table 4)',{'Nature':'Archer (ref. 4, Table 4)','Science':'Archer (1, Table 4)','National_Science_Review':'Archer [1, Table 4]','Icarus':'Archer (2023, Table 4)'}[venue])
 # Insert immediately after Introduction without changing earlier summary.

# Conference documents.
abstract=('Fresh-crater relief and a conserved water budget provide complementary tests of early Martian environments. I compare the elevation-bin means reported by Archer (2023) for 2–5 km fresh craters with an atmospheric-entry surrogate spanning 0.5–3 bar, and separately reconstruct conditional subsurface water domains on Mars and Earth from 4.4 Ga to the present. The fresh-only inversion assigns 58.5% of its discrete pressure weight to 3 bar, the highest pressure tested. This boundary result depends on entry physics, nuisance priors and assumed errors; morphological freshness does not fix impact age. It therefore motivates expanded pressure and fragmentation tests rather than a unique Noachian pressure estimate. In the reference water scenario at 3.8 Ga, Mars retains 731.1 m of unbound global equivalent layer. A hydrostatic pure-water screen admits 484.2 m of potential liquid, including 115.5 m within 0–80 °C. That restricted water volume is 1.6674 × 10¹⁶ m³, with a mean temperature of 34.8 °C. The suitability-weighted mean depth is 2.98 km and operational pore closure is 22.43 km. Absolute suitable-water volume peaks at the oldest modeled frame, while the fraction normalized to assigned pore water peaks near the present. Both planets already contain suitable domains at 4.4 Ga, leaving onset precedence unresolved. Atmospheric fits do not constrain subsurface saturation or independently validate the thermal model. Together these results define testable requirements for an early Martian microbial nursery while leaving biological ancestry and successful lithopanspermia unestablished.')
for conf in ['EGU','LPSC']:
 doc=Document();sec=doc.sections[0];sec.top_margin=sec.bottom_margin=sec.left_margin=sec.right_margin=Inches(1);sec.page_width=Inches(8.5);sec.page_height=Inches(11)
 doc.styles['Normal'].font.name='Times New Roman';doc.styles['Normal'].font.size=Pt(11)
 doc.add_heading('Fresh craters, atmospheric scenarios and water-limited Martian habitats',0);doc.add_paragraph('Richard Archer · Atlas AstroBioTek')
 if conf=='EGU':doc.add_paragraph('AUTHOR-REVISION DRAFT. EGU27 prohibits AI creation of substantive abstract content. The author must substantively rewrite this text before submission; language editing alone does not resolve its provenance.')

 doc.save(D/(f'{conf}_2027_Abstract_v3'+('_AUTHOR_REVISION.docx' if conf=='EGU' else '.docx')))

"""Native Office Math Markup Language authoring; equations remain editable."""
from copy import deepcopy
from docx.oxml import OxmlElement
from docx.oxml.ns import qn
from docx.enum.text import WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH

def node(tag,*children):
 e=OxmlElement('m:'+tag)
 for ch in children:
  if isinstance(ch,str):e.append(run(ch))
  elif isinstance(ch,(list,tuple)):
   for c in ch:e.append(run(c) if isinstance(c,str) else deepcopy(c))
  else:e.append(deepcopy(ch))
 return e
def run(text):
 e=OxmlElement('m:r');pr=OxmlElement('w:rPr');font=OxmlElement('w:rFonts')
 font.set(qn('w:ascii'),'Cambria Math');font.set(qn('w:hAnsi'),'Cambria Math');pr.append(font);e.append(pr)
 
 if str(text).strip() in ['exp','clip','arg max']:
  mr=OxmlElement('m:rPr');sty=OxmlElement('m:sty');sty.set(qn('m:val'),'p');mr.append(sty);e.insert(0,mr)
 t=OxmlElement('m:t');t.text=str(text);t.set(qn('xml:space'),'preserve');e.append(t);return e
def seq(*parts):return list(parts)
def sub(base,script):return node('sSub',node('e',base),node('sub',script))
def sup(base,script):return node('sSup',node('e',base),node('sup',script))
def frac(n,d):return node('f',node('num',n),node('den',d))
def integral(expr,low='0',high='zₘₐₓ',char='∫'):
 pr=node('naryPr');c=OxmlElement('m:chr');c.set(qn('m:val'),char);pr.append(c)
 loc=OxmlElement('m:limLoc');loc.set(qn('m:val'),'subSup');pr.append(loc)
 if high=='':
  hide=OxmlElement('m:supHide');hide.set(qn('m:val'),'1');pr.append(hide)
 return node('nary',pr,node('sub',low),node('sup',high),node('e',expr))
def summ(expr,index='d'):return integral(expr,index,'',char='∑')
def par(expr):
 pr=node('dPr');a=OxmlElement('m:begChr');a.set(qn('m:val'),'(');b=OxmlElement('m:endChr');b.set(qn('m:val'),')');pr.extend([a,b])
 return node('d',pr,node('e',expr))

Ai=sub('A','i');qs=sub('q','s');qb=sub('q','b');Ts=sub('T','s');Wp=sub('W','p');Vl=sub('V','ℓ');Vl80=sub('V','ℓ,0–80');Ap=sub('A','p')
EQ={
1:seq(Ai,'(a) = ',Ai,'(0) ',sup('2',frac('a',sub('t','½,i')))),
2:seq(qs,'(a) = ',qb,'(a) + A(a)L'),
3:seq('T(z,a) = ',Ts,'(a) + ',frac(seq(qs,'(a)z − ',frac(seq('A(a)',sup('z','2')),'2')),'k')),
4:seq(qb,'(a) = ',qb,'(0) ', 'exp', par('ca')),
5:seq(sub('W','u'),'(a) = ',sub('W','0'),' + D(a) − B(a) − E(a)'),
6:seq(sub('F','XUV'),'(a,r) = ',sub('F','0'),sup(par(frac(sub('a','☉,0'),seq(sub('a','☉,0'),' − a'))),'1.23'),sup(par(frac('1 AU','r')),'2')),
7:seq('s(T) = ', 'clip',par(seq(frac('T','10 °C'),', 0, 1')),' ', 'clip',par(seq(frac('80 °C − T','15 °C'),', 0, 1'))),
8:seq(Wp,' = ',summ(seq(sub('A','d'),' ',integral(seq(sub('φ','d'),' ',sub('S','w,d'),' dz'))))),
9:seq('H = ',summ(seq(sub('A','d'),' ',integral(seq(sub('φ','d'),' ',sub('S','w,d'),' s(T) dz'))))),
10:seq(Vl,' = ',summ(seq(sub('A','d'),' ',integral(seq(sub('φ','d'),' ',sub('S','w,d'),' ',sub('I','ℓ'),' dz'))))),
11:seq(sub('h','GEL'),' = ',frac(Vl,Ap),',    1 km³ = ',sup('10','9'),' m³'),
12:seq(sub('f','p'),' = ',frac('H',Wp),',    ',sub('f','ℓ'),' = ',frac('H',Vl),',    ',sub('f','0–80'),' = ',frac('H',Vl80)),
13:seq(sub('z̄','H'),' = ',frac(integral('z dH'),'H'),',    ',sub('z','*'),' = ',sub('arg max','z'),par(frac('dH','dz'))),
14:seq(sub('T̄','ℓ'),' = ',frac(integral(seq('T d',Vl)),Vl)),
15:seq(sub('p','j'),' = ',frac(sub('n','j'),'N'),',    ',sub('V','del,j'),' = ',sub('p','j'),' ',sub('V','ej')),
16:seq(sub('V̇','j'),' = ',frac(sub('V','del,j'),'Δt')),
17:seq(sub('D','XUV'),'(d) = ',frac(seq(sub('F̄','XUV'),' ',sub('t','tr')),'4 ρ δ'),' exp',par(frac('−d','δ'))),
18:seq(sub('D','GCR'),'(d) = ',sub('Ḋ','0'),' ',sub('t','tr'),' exp',par(frac('−ρd','Λ'))),
19:seq(sub('f','D*'),' = 1 − ',sup(par(frac('R − d*','R')),'3'),',    0 ≤ d* ≤ R'),
20:seq(sub('λ','j'),' = N ',sub('f','i'),' ',sub('f','e'),' ',sub('p','j'),' exp',par(frac(seq('−',sub('t','j')),'τ')),' ',sub('p','a'),' ',sub('I','j')),
21:seq(sub('P','j'),' = 1 − ', 'exp', par(seq('−',sub('λ','j')))),
22:seq(sub('P','pore'),'(z) = ',sub('P','s'),' + ',sub('ρ','w'),'gz ≥ ',sub('P','sat'),'(T)'),
23:seq(sub('d','*'),' = sup{d ∈ [0,R] : ',sub('D','XUV'),'(d) + ',sub('D','GCR'),'(d) ≥ D*}'),
24:seq(integral(seq('d',Vl80),'0',sub('z','q')),' = q ',Vl80,',    q ∈ {0.1,0.9}')}
DESCRIPTIONS={1:'Equivalent isotope inventory',2:'Surface conductive heat flux',3:'Conductive temperature profile',4:'Assumed basal flux evolution',5:'Conserved Martian unbound water budget',6:'Young-Sun XUV forcing',7:'Comparative thermal suitability score',8:'Assigned free pore water',9:'Suitable-water equivalent',10:'Potential liquid water volume',11:'Global equivalent layer and volume units',12:'Three explicitly different normalizations',13:'Mean and modal habitat depth',14:'Liquid-volume-weighted mean temperature',15:'Conditional transfer volume',16:'Ensemble-averaged normalized exchange rate',17:'Absorbed XUV-dose surrogate',18:'Radial particle-dose surrogate',19:'Spherical volume above a dose threshold',20:'Expected established inocula',21:'Conditional establishment probability',22:'Hydrostatic pure-water phase screen',23:'Dose-threshold shell depth',24:'Lens depth quantiles'}

def equation(d,n):
 p=d.add_paragraph();p.alignment=WD_ALIGN_PARAGRAPH.CENTER;p.paragraph_format.keep_together=True
 e=node('oMath',EQ[n]);p._p.append(e);p.add_run(f'   ({n})')
 return p

def inline(p,*parts):p._p.append(node('oMath',parts))

def para(c,text,x,y,w,size=18,color='#183443',leading=None):
 p=Paragraph(text,ParagraphStyle('x',fontName='DejaVu',fontSize=size,leading=leading or size*1.3,textColor=HexColor(color)));_,h=p.wrap(w,10000);p.drawOn(c,x,y-h);return y-h

def img(c,path,x,y,w,h):
 im=ImageReader(str(path));iw,ih=im.getSize();s=min(w/iw,h/ih);dw,dh=iw*s,ih*s;c.drawImage(im,x+(w-dw)/2,y+(h-dh)/2,dw,dh,mask='auto')

('Could early Mars have supported a microbial nursery?','Figure_13_Riverbank_Panorama.png','Richard Archer<br/>Atlas AstroBioTek<br/><br/>An evidence-led journey through craters, water and planetary exchange','Open with the landscape as an artistic invitation. Ask the audience to imagine standing beside this river. Explain that the picture is an illustration, while the following graphs distinguish observations from calculations. The central question concerns the physical opportunity for life. No Martian organism has been demonstrated. Pause for the audience to inspect the waterfalls and icy highland before moving to the measurements.'),
('Craters preserve a climate record','Figure_12_Original_Landscape.png','Archer (2023): small craters, elevation and hydrological landforms','Introduce the published work in Icarus 406, article 115734. Describe a crater rim and floor, then explain why erosion, infilling and initial impact conditions can all change relief. Fresh means morphologically well preserved. It does not mean that every crater formed at one time. The published study examined Noachian terrain, but a crater formed within old terrain can itself be younger. This chronological distinction matters for every atmospheric claim that follows.'),
('Fresh-crater relief changes with elevation','Public_Fresh_2_3km.png','24 published means<br/>Three crater-size groups<br/>Eight elevation bins','Spend the first half of this slide teaching the axes.
