"""FRESH-ONLY Bayesian inversion: atmospheric pressure, gas mixture, impactor class.
Uses only Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh-crater elevation-bin means.
No degraded craters, erosion, valley networks, or climate-model priors enter likelihood.
NO independent meteorite classification is possible from these data.
No fragmentation/ablation hydrocode: strength is used only as a survival filter.
Run: python mars_impactor_class_joint.py
"""
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np, pandas as pd
import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from scipy.special import logsumexp
from scipy.stats import qmc,norm
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'crater_analysis'
raw=pd.read_csv(ROOT/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')
classes=['2-3','3-4','4-5']; mids=np.array([2.5,3.5,4.5]); z=np.sort(raw.elevation_km.unique())
def matrix(col):
 return np.array([raw[raw.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)].set_index('elevation_km').reindex(z)[col].to_numpy(float) for c in classes])
fresh=matrix('fresh_depth_km')
fsd=matrix('fresh_sd_km')
SF=np.sqrt((fsd/np.sqrt(20))**2+.02**2)
pressures=[.5,1.,1.5,2.,3.]; h2s=[0.,.01,.03,.05,.07,.10]; rhs=[.35,.75]
# Candidate physical *analogs*, not meteorite spectral classifications.
meteorites={
 'Carbonaceous chondrite':(1800,900,0.8),
 'Ordinary chondrite':(3400,5e6,1.0),
 'Stony-iron':(4700,2e7,1.0),
 'Iron':(7800,1e8,1.0)}
# strength [Pa] are broad nominal survival thresholds, not measured Noachian materials.
N=64; u=np.clip(qmc.Sobol(d=10,scramble=True,seed=3852023).random_base2(6),1e-9,1-1e-9)
g=3.71; T=np.clip(270+12*norm.ppf(u[:,0]),235,300)
angle=np.arcsin(np.sqrt(.08+.9*u[:,1])); density_factor=np.exp(.18*norm.ppf(u[:,2]))
relief_ratio=np.exp(np.log(.105)+.10*norm.ppf(u[:,3])); diameter_scale=np.exp(.12*norm.ppf(u[:,4]))
# Size distribution: differential dN/dD~D^-3.5; integrated logarithmic bins ~D^-2.5.
D=np.geomspace(35,1400,75)[None,:,None]
V=np.array([7000.,10000.,13000.,17000.,22000.])[None,None,:]
weights=(D/100)**(-2.5)*np.array([.12,.25,.29,.22,.12])[None,None,:]
weights=np.broadcast_to(weights,(1,75,5)); weights=weights/weights.sum()
def vapor_pressure(t):
 c=t-273.15
 return 611.21*np.exp((18.678-c/234.5)*(c/(257.14+c)))
records=[]; per_bin=[]; best_by_pressure={}
for p in pressures:
 for h in h2s:
  for rh in rhs:
   water=np.minimum(.015,rh*vapor_pressure(T)/(p*1e5)); co2=1-h-water
   R=co2*188.9+h*4124+water*461.5; H=R*T/g
   for name,(density,strength,cd) in meteorites.items():
    initial=np.full((N,3,len(z)),np.nan); survival=np.zeros((N,3,len(z)))
    rho_i=density*density_factor
    for k,alt in enumerate(z):
     column=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/g
     beta=(2/3)*rho_i[:,None,None]*D/cd
     v=V*np.exp(-column[:,None,None]/(2*beta*np.sin(angle[:,None,None])))
     # Approximate fragmentation flag: dynamic pressure at max atmospheric density
     # is NOT a validated breakup model. Reduced weight, rather than hard exclusion.
     rho_air=p*1e5*np.exp(-alt*1000/H)/(R*T)
     q=.5*rho_air[:,None,None]*v*v
     survive=np.exp(-np.maximum(q/strength-1,0)*.7)
     crater=1.3*1.16*diameter_scale[:,None,None]*(rho_i[:,None,None]/3000)**(1/3)*D**.78*(v*v/g)**.22/1000
     for j,(lo,hi) in enumerate([(2,3),(3,4),(4,5)]):
      ww=weights*survive*((crater>=lo)&(crater<hi))
      normw=ww.sum(axis=(1,2))
      dmean=np.divide((ww*crater).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      vmean=np.divide((ww*v).sum(axis=(1,2)),normw,out=np.full(N,np.nan),where=normw>0)
      initial[:,j,k]=relief_ratio*dmean*(vmean/12000)**.15
      survival[:,j,k]=normw
    residual_f=((initial-fresh[None,:,:])/SF[None,:,:])**2
    lf=-.5*np.sum(residual_f,axis=(1,2))
    lf[~np.isfinite(initial).all(axis=(1,2))]=-np.inf
    evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)
    ix=int(np.argmax(lf))
    if np.isfinite(lf[ix]) and (p not in best_by_pressure or lf[ix]>best_by_pressure[p]['ll']):
     best_by_pressure[p]={'ll':float(lf[ix]),'prediction':initial[ix].copy(),'h2':h,'rh':rh,'class':name}
    records.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,h2o_pct=100*water.mean(),co2_pct=100*co2.mean(),
       relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,log_evidence=evidence))
    for j,crater_bin in enumerate(classes):
      lfj=-.5*np.sum(residual_f[:,j,:],axis=1)
      lfj[~np.isfinite(initial[:,j,:]).all(axis=1)]=-np.inf
      per_bin.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,relative_humidity=rh,meteorite_class=name,
          crater_bin_km=crater_bin,log_evidence=logsumexp(lfj)-np.log(N)))
res=pd.DataFrame(records); res['posterior']=np.exp(res.log_evidence-logsumexp(res.log_evidence))
res.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_grid.csv',index=False)
summary=[]
for col in ['pressure_bar','meteorite_class','h2_pct','relative_humidity']:
 tab=res.groupby(col,as_index=False).posterior.sum().rename(columns={'posterior':'probability'})
 tab.to_csv(ROOT/f'mars_fresh_only_posterior_{col}.csv',index=False)
 summary.append((col,tab))
binres=pd.DataFrame(per_bin)
for crater_bin,part in binres.groupby('crater_bin_km'):
 part=part.copy();part['posterior']=np.exp(part.log_evidence-logsumexp(part.log_evidence))
 binres.loc[part.index,'within_bin_posterior']=part.posterior
bybin=binres.groupby(['crater_bin_km','meteorite_class'],as_index=False).within_bin_posterior.sum()
bybin.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_class_by_crater_bin.csv',index=False)
# PCA includes pressure, impactor density, and gas composition; gases sum to one.
X=res[['pressure_bar','co2_pct','h2_pct','h2o_pct']].copy()
X['impactor_density_kg_m3']=res.meteorite_class.map({k:v[0] for k,v in meteorites.items()})
scaler=StandardScaler();scaled=scaler.fit_transform(X)
pca=PCA(n_components=4).fit(scaled)
load=pd.DataFrame(pca.components_.T,index=X.columns,columns=[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)])
load.to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_loadings.csv')
pd.DataFrame({'component':[f'PC{i+1}' for i in range(4)],'variance_fraction':pca.explained_variance_ratio_}).to_csv(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_pca_variance.csv',index=False)
fig,axs=plt.subplots(2,2,figsize=(12,9),layout='constrained')
pr=dict(summary)['pressure_bar'];axs[0,0].bar(pr.pressure_bar.astype(str),pr.probability,color='#477eac');axs[0,0].set(title='Pressure posterior',xlabel='bar',ylabel='Probability')
cl=dict(summary)['meteorite_class'];axs[0,1].barh(cl.meteorite_class,cl.probability,color='#788d79');axs[0,1].set(title='Impactor-class posterior',xlabel='Probability')
pivot=bybin.pivot(index='crater_bin_km',columns='meteorite_class',values='within_bin_posterior').loc[classes]
pivot.plot(kind='bar',ax=axs[1,0]);axs[1,0].set(title='Class ranking by final crater diameter',xlabel='Final crater diameter (km)',ylabel='Within-bin probability');axs[1,0].legend(fontsize=7)
axs[1,1].bar(np.arange(4)+1,pca.explained_variance_ratio_,color='#7c87a6');axs[1,1].set(title='PCA of pressure, gases and impactor density',xlabel='Principal component',ylabel='Variance explained')
fig.suptitle('Fresh-crater-only inversion: atmospheric pressure and impactor classes\nClass priors and simplified survival law strongly affect rankings',fontsize=12)
fig.savefig(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_joint_figure.png',dpi=180,bbox_inches='tight');plt.close(fig)
with open(ROOT/'mars_fresh_only_methodology.txt','w') as f:
 f.write('EXPLORATORY MODEL, NOT METEORITE IDENTIFICATION\n\n')
 f.write('Uses ONLY Archer (2023) Table 4 fresh crater relief elevation-bin means.\n')
 f.write('NO degraded crater data, NO valley networks, NO climate/erosion models enter the likelihood.\n')
 f.write('Equal class, pressure, H2 and humidity grid priors.\n')
 f.write('Crater gravity-regime scaling and impactor survival are simplified; ablation, fragmentation,\n')
 f.write('impactor size-frequency distributions and rock strengths are poorly constrained.\n')
 f.write('Likelihood assumes effective crater count 20 and uncertainty floors 20/30 meters.\n')
 f.write('The highest posterior class is conditional on these assumptions and cannot identify a meteorite class.\n')
 f.write('PCA is descriptive, not causal. Gas fractions sum to 100%, creating dependence.\n')
 f.write('The paper has 2-3, 3-4, 4-5 km bins; no 1-2 km data are present.\n')
print('PRESSURE',dict(summary)['pressure_bar'].to_string(index=False))
print('CLASS',cl.to_string(index=False))
print('BY BIN',bybin.to_string(index=False))
print('PCA',pca.explained_variance_ratio_)

fitrows=[]
for p,b in best_by_pressure.items():
 for j,c in enumerate(classes):
  for k,alt in enumerate(z):fitrows.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,diameter_bin_km=c,elevation_km=alt,observed_depth_km=fresh[j,k],sigma_km=SF[j,k],predicted_depth_km=b['prediction'][j,k],h2_pct=100*b['h2'],relative_humidity=b['rh'],impactor_analog=b['class'],chi2=-2*b['ll']))
pd.DataFrame(fitrows).to_csv(ROOT/'fresh_best_fit_by_pressure.csv',index=False)


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/crater_observations/audit_spatial_components.py
================================================================================
from pathlib import Path
from collections import defaultdict
import csv,hashlib,struct,zipfile
root=Path('/mnt/data'); out=root/'archer_2023_combined_analysis';out.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
files=sorted(root.glob('*.shx')); rows=[]; byhash=defaultdict(list)
for f in files:
 b=f.read_bytes(); digest=hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest();byhash[digest].append(f.name)
 valid=len(b)>=100 and struct.unpack('>i',b[0:4])[0]==9994 and (len(b)-100)%8==0
 n=(len(b)-100)//8 if valid else None
 name=f.stem
 # match exact names, strip Windows duplicate suffixes and Copy, preserve A distinction
 import re
 base=re.sub(r'\(\d+\)$','',name);base=re.sub(r' - Copy$','',base)
 matches=[x for x in root.glob('*.dbf') if x.stem.lower()==base.lower()]
 if not matches and base=='InvVNN0p5shp':matches=list(root.glob('InvVNN0p5shp*.dbf'))
 dbf=matches[0] if matches else None
 dbf_n=struct.unpack('<I',dbf.read_bytes()[4:8])[0] if dbf else None
 rows.append(dict(shx_file=f.name,sha256=digest,shx_record_count=n,valid_shx_header=valid,canonical_stem=base,matched_dbf=dbf.name if dbf else '',dbf_record_count=dbf_n,record_count_agrees=(n==dbf_n if dbf else ''),shp_present=bool(list(root.glob(base+'.shp'))),duplicate_shx_copies=''))
for r in rows:r['duplicate_shx_copies']='; '.join(x for x in byhash[r['sha256']] if x!=r['shx_file'])
with (out/'spatial_component_audit.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
 w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=rows[0].keys());w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
with (out/'SPATIAL_DATA_README.txt').open('w') as f:
 f.write('ARCHER (2023) UPDATED SPATIAL COMPONENT AUDIT\n\n')
 f.write(f'Uploaded: {len(list(root.glob("*.dbf")))} DBF, {len(files)} SHX, {len(list(root.glob("*.shp")))} SHP.\n')
 f.write(f'SHX distinct file hashes: {len(byhash)}; repeated copies: {len(files)-len(byhash)}.\n')
 f.write(f'SHX matching DBF record counts: {sum(r["record_count_agrees"] is True for r in rows)} / {sum(bool(r["matched_dbf"]) for r in rows)}.\n')
 f.write('IMPORTANT: SHX is an index, not spatial geometry. No .shp files were uploaded.\n')
 f.write('Consequently no geospatial reconstruction, accurate channel geometry/lengths, areal densities, or spatially explicit GCM validation can be derived from these SHX files.\n')
 f.write('Prior cleaned CSVs and plots are first-pass DBF-based exploratory outputs, NOT final climate hypothesis testing.\n')
 f.write('Original input DBF data were not modified. SHX duplicates were identified by SHA-256, not merged into crater records.\n')
 f.write('For a spatial analysis, provide matching .shp files (and .prj files when available) plus per-elevation Noachian terrain areas.\n')
 f.write('The Python script build_archer_analysis.py is retained to reproduce the first-pass analysis.\n')
archive=root/'archer_2023_combined_results_updated.zip'
with zipfile.ZipFile(archive,'w',zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as z:
 for p in sorted(out.glob('*')):z.write(p,arcname=p.name)
 for p in [root/'build_archer_analysis.py',root/'audit_spatial_components.py']:z.write(p,arcname=p.name)
print('shx',len(files),'unique',len(byhash),'matched',sum(bool(r['matched_dbf']) for r in rows),'count agree',sum(r['record_count_agrees'] is True for r in rows),'zip',archive,archive.stat().st_size)
print('mismatches',[(r['shx_file'],r['shx_record_count'],r['dbf_record_count']) for r in rows if r['matched_dbf'] and not r['record_count_agrees']][:20])


================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/crater_observations/build_archer_analysis.py
================================================================================
import os,glob,re,json,zipfile,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd,shapefile
from scipy import stats
import matplotlib; matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
ROOT=Path('/mnt/data'); OUT=ROOT/'archer_2023_combined_analysis';OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
files=sorted(ROOT.glob('*.dbf'))
cr=[]; inv=[]; audit=[]
for f in files:
 r=shapefile.Reader(dbf=str(f),encoding='latin1'); cols=[a[0] for a in r.fields[1:]]
 typ='inverted_channel' if f.name.startswith('InvVN') else ('fresh' if 'Fresh' in f.name else 'degraded')
 mm=re.search(r'(?:Neg|UP|InvVNN|InvVNU)(\d)p5',f.name)
 mid=(int(mm.group(1))+.5)*(-1 if f.name.startswith(('Neg','InvVNN')) else 1) if mm else np.nan
 count=0
 for i,record in enumerate(r.iterRecords()):
  d=dict(zip(cols,record)); d.update(source_file=f.name,source_row=i+1,file_class=typ,file_bin_mid_km=mid)
  (inv if typ=='inverted_channel' else cr).append(d);count+=1
 audit.append(dict(file=f.name,type=typ,bin_center_km=mid,records=count,fields=';'.join(cols)))
pd.DataFrame(audit).to_csv(OUT/'input_inventory.csv',index=False)
c=pd.DataFrame(cr);v=pd.DataFrame(inv)
for x in ['DIAM_CIRCL','DEPTH_RIMF','DEGRADATIO','LATITUDE_C','LONGITUDE_','OBJECTID']:
 c[x]=pd.to_numeric(c[x],errors='coerce')
c['crater_id_key']=c['CRATER_ID'].astype(str).str.strip()
c['class_from_grade']=np.select([c.DEGRADATIO.isin([1,2]),c.DEGRADATIO.isin([3,4])],['degraded','fresh'],default='unknown')
c['file_grade_disagreement']=c.file_class.ne(c.class_from_grade)
c['diameter_bin']=pd.cut(c.DIAM_CIRCL,[2,3,4,5],right=False,labels=['2-3','3-4','4-5'])
c['in_scope_2_5km']=c.DIAM_CIRCL.ge(2)&c.DIAM_CIRCL.lt(5)
# Preserve original files as converted CSV and full audit
for f,grp in c.groupby('source_file',sort=True):grp.to_csv(OUT/(f+'.csv'),index=False)
for f,grp in v.groupby('source_file',sort=True):grp.to_csv(OUT/(f+'.csv'),index=False)
# Dedup on stable crater id, separately log conflicts. Do not collapse classification contradictions silently.
c['key']=np.where(c.crater_id_key.str.len()>2,c.crater_id_key,'loc_'+c.LATITUDE_C.round(3).astype(str)+'_'+c['LONGITUDE_'].round(3).astype(str)+'_'+c.DIAM_CIRCL.round(2).astype(str))
c['key_occurrences']=c.groupby('key')['key'].transform('size')
c['key_class_count']=c.groupby('key')['class_from_grade'].transform('nunique')
c['grade_priority']=np.where(c.class_from_grade.eq(c.file_class),0,1)
c=c.sort_values(['key','grade_priority','source_file','source_row'])
duplicates=c[c.duplicated('key',keep='first')].copy(); duplicates.to_csv(OUT/'removed_duplicate_crater_rows.csv',index=False)
conflicts=c[c.key_class_count>1].copy();conflicts.to_csv(OUT/'crater_classification_conflicts.csv',index=False)
unique=c.drop_duplicates('key',keep='first').copy()
unique['analysis_class']=np.where(unique.key_class_count>1,'conflicted',unique.class_from_grade)
unique.to_csv(OUT/'all_craters_deduplicated.csv',index=False)
unique[unique.analysis_class=='fresh'].to_csv(OUT/'fresh_craters_clean.csv',index=False)
unique[unique.analysis_class=='degraded'].to_csv(OUT/'degraded_craters_clean.csv',index=False)
# Inverted channel Id is NOT a unique key. Geometry is unavailable in DBF-only upload.
for x in ['Length','Z_Mean','Latitude','Longitude']:v[x]=pd.to_numeric(v[x],errors='coerce')
v['inferred_elevation_km']=v.Z_Mean/1000
v['actual_bin_mid_km']=np.floor(v.inferred_elevation_km)+0.5
v['bin_mismatch']=~np.isclose(v.actual_bin_mid_km,v.file_bin_mid_km)
# exact geometry-attribute duplicate only, retain separate features with identical centroid if length differs
v['fingerprint']=v[['Length','Z_Mean','Latitude','Longitude','Morphology']].astype(str).agg('|'.join,axis=1)
v=v.sort_values(['source_file','source_row'])
v[v.duplicated('fingerprint')].to_csv(OUT/'removed_duplicate_channel_rows.csv',index=False)
v=v.drop_duplicates('fingerprint')
v.to_csv(OUT/'inverted_channels_deduplicated.csv',index=False)
# Aggregate. In absence of AOI area per elevation interval, fractions NOT spatial densities.
a=unique[unique.analysis_class.isin(['fresh','degraded']) & unique.in_scope_2_5km].copy()
counts=a.groupby(['file_bin_mid_km','diameter_bin','analysis_class'],observed=True).size().unstack(fill_value=0).reset_index()
for cls in ['fresh','degraded']:
 if cls not in counts:counts[cls]=0
counts['total']=counts.fresh+counts.degraded
counts['fresh_fraction']=counts.fresh/counts.total
counts['degraded_to_fresh']=counts.degraded/counts.fresh.replace(0,np.nan)
counts.to_csv(OUT/'crater_elevation_diameter_counts.csv',index=False)
depths=a.groupby(['file_bin_mid_km','diameter_bin','analysis_class'],observed=True).agg(n=('DEPTH_RIMF','count'),mean_depth_km=('DEPTH_RIMF','mean'),sd_depth_km=('DEPTH_RIMF','std'),mean_diameter_km=('DIAM_CIRCL','mean')).reset_index()
depths['sem_depth_km']=depths.sd_depth_km/np.sqrt(depths.n)
depths.to_csv(OUT/'crater_depth_statistics.csv',index=False)
chan=v.groupby('file_bin_mid_km').agg(n=('Length','size'),sum_length_field=('Length','sum'),mean_length_field=('Length','mean'),mean_actual_elev_km=('inferred_elevation_km','mean'),bin_mismatch=('bin_mismatch','sum')).reset_index()
chan.to_csv(OUT/'inverted_channel_bin_statistics.csv',index=False)
# Published Archer Appendix A Table 2 coefficients, in km. x = median elevation in km.
published={
 'fresh_2-3':[.20883,.003521], 'fresh_3-4':[.36905,.0032369], 'fresh_4-5':[.47525,.0035797],
 'degraded_2-3':[.13617,.016771,.0030185,-.0024544], 'degraded_3-4':[.17843,.019265,-8.7861e-6,-.0053494], 'degraded_4-5':[.20872,.010936,.0039197,-.0049133],
 'difference_2-3':[.061556,-.016058,-.0014475,-.0029429], 'difference_3-4':[.19947,.01059852,-.0022454,.0026611], 'difference_4-5':[.2715,.0034564,-.0052407,.0035734],
 'VN_below_0p5':[.027861,.010009], 'VN_above_0p5':[.036326,-.0045988], 'VN_plus_inverted_below_0p5':[.028787,.0095978],
 'inverted_all':[.00081826,-.0005588,.00020233,-3.456e-5,-5.9573e-5],
 'deg_fresh_ratio_vs_VN_below_neg0p5':[2.0544,-39.182], 'deg_fresh_ratio_vs_VN_above_neg0p5':[.6224,17.554]
}
pd.DataFrame([{'relation':k,'coefficients_low_to_high':json.dumps(val)} for k,val in published.items()]).to_csv(OUT/'archer_published_equations.csv',index=False)
# Compare observed mean depths with published regressions, WITHOUT claiming exact reproduction of original table
rows=[]
for _,row in depths.iterrows():
 key=f"{row.analysis_class}_{row.diameter_bin}"
 if key not in published:continue
 x=row.file_bin_mid_km;co=published[key];pred=sum(t*x**j for j,t in enumerate(co))
 rows.append(dict(elevation_mid_km=x,diameter_bin=row.diameter_bin,crater_class=row.analysis_class,n=row.n,observed_depth_km=row.mean_depth_km,published_fit_km=pred,residual_km=row.mean_depth_km-pred))
compare=pd.DataFrame(rows);compare.to_csv(OUT/'published_vs_uploaded_depths.csv',index=False)
# Conditional odds and regression; NOT Bayesian climate hypothesis posterior.
reg=[]
for diam,group in counts.groupby('diameter_bin',observed=True):
 g=group[(group.total>0)&(group.file_bin_mid_km.between(-3.5,3.5))]
 if len(g)>2:
  # binomial GLM using statsmodels when available
  try:
   import statsmodels.api as sm
   fit=sm.GLM(g.fresh_fraction,sm.add_constant(g.file_bin_mid_km),family=sm.families.Binomial(),freq_weights=g.total).fit()
   reg.append(dict(diameter_bin=diam,log_odds_per_km=fit.params.iloc[1],odds_ratio_per_km=np.exp(fit.params.iloc[1]),p_value=fit.pvalues.iloc[1],n=int(g.total.sum()),model='binomial_glm_no_area_offset'))
  except Exception as e:reg.append(dict(diameter_bin=diam,error=str(e)))
pd.DataFrame(reg).to_csv(OUT/'fresh_preservation_elevation_glm.csv',index=False)
# physical sensitivity table: pressure, hydrogen, temperature, scale height
sens=[]
for p in [.5,1,1.5,2,3]:
 for h in [0,.05,.10,.15,.20,.25]:
  mm=(1-h)*44.01+h*2.016
  for T in [230,250,273,283]:
   H=8.314462618*T/(mm/1000*3.71)/1000
   sens.append(dict(surface_pressure_bar=p,h2_mole_fraction=h,co2_mole_fraction=1-h,T_K=T,mean_molar_mass_g_mol=mm,scale_height_km=H,p_at_5km_bar=p*np.exp(-5/H),p_at_10km_bar=p*np.exp(-10/H)))
pd.DataFrame(sens).to_csv(OUT/'atmosphere_scale_height_sensitivity.csv',index=False)
# plots
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(9,5))
for diam,g in counts.groupby('diameter_bin',observed=True):ax.plot(g.file_bin_mid_km,g.fresh_fraction,'o-',label=str(diam)+' km')
ax.axvline(1,color='grey',ls='--',label='Proposed LNIH ELA');ax.set(xlabel='Elevation bin center (km)',ylabel='Fresh fraction among classified 2–5 km craters',title='Crater preservation by elevation and diameter');ax.legend();fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(OUT/'crater_fresh_fraction.png',dpi=160);plt.close(fig)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(9,5))
for (cls,diam),g in depths.groupby(['analysis_class','diameter_bin'],observed=True):
 if len(g):ax.plot(g.file_bin_mid_km,g.mean_depth_km,'o-',label=f'{cls} {diam} km')
ax.axvline(1,color='grey',ls='--');ax.set(xlabel='Elevation bin center (km)',ylabel='Mean rim-floor relief (km)',title='Uploaded crater relief by elevation');ax.legend(fontsize=8,ncol=2);fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(OUT/'crater_relief.png',dpi=160);plt.close(fig)
fig,ax=plt.subplots(figsize=(9,5));ax.bar(chan.file_bin_mid_km,chan.n,width=.7);ax.set(xlabel='File elevation bin center (km)',ylabel='Inverted channel DBF record count',title='Inverted-channel record counts (not areal densities)');fig.tight_layout();fig.savefig(OUT/'inverted_channel_counts.png',dpi=160);plt.close(fig)
summary=dict(input_files=len(files),input_crater_rows=len(c),unique_craters=len(unique),removed_crater_duplicates=len(duplicates),conflicted_unique_craters=int((unique.analysis_class=='conflicted').sum()),file_grade_disagreement_rows=int(c.file_grade_disagreement.sum()),unique_fresh=int((unique.analysis_class=='fresh').sum()),unique_degraded=int((unique.analysis_class=='degraded').sum()),input_inverted_channel_rows=len(inv),unique_inverted_channel_rows=len(v),inverted_channel_bin_mismatch=int(v.bin_mismatch.sum()),crater_analysis_rows_2_to_5km=len(a),note='DBF files have attributes only; spatial geometries and bin-specific terrain areas not provided. No actual 3D GCM simulation or calibrated Bayesian climate posterior performed.')
(OUT/'analysis_summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
(OUT/'README.txt').write_text('ARCHER 2023 COUPLED ANALYSIS - REPRODUCIBLE ATTRIBUTES AND DIAGNOSTICS\n\n'+json.dumps(summary,indent=2)+'\n\nMethods: exact crater ID deduplication; class derived from DEGRADATIO (1/2 degraded, 3/4 fresh); conflicting class excluded from analysis, retained in master CSV; duplicated channel records identified by identical length, Z_Mean, lat, lon, morphology. Bin elevation from filename, and actual inverted-channel Z_Mean also retained. The source files lack shapefile .shp geometry and elevation-bin terrain areas; thus no physically justified VN length per area or crater density per area. Fresh/degraded ratios are valid within matched sampled bins if samples share selection masks. Published coefficients transcribed from Archer (2023) Appendix A Table 2; regressions not refit to figures. Negative/positive upper bins may overlap by user-provided definitions, inspect bin mismatch audit. Model atmosphere scale heights are ideal-gas diagnostics only, not climate simulations. No calibration of erosion K, no net water balance, no likelihood-based climate hypothesis ranking without GCM scenario outputs.\n')
with zipfile.ZipFile(ROOT/'archer_2023_combined_results.zip','w',compression=zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED,compresslevel=6) as z:
 for f in OUT.iterdir():z.write(f,arcname=f.name)
print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2));print('OUTPUTS',len(list(OUT.iterdir())))

================================================================================
research/mars_earth_foundation/Mars_NoachianHabitability_Final_v3/source_model/source/thermal_foundation.py
================================================================================
"""Reproducible conditional aqueous-habitat scenarios, reviewed 2026-10-31.
Run: python build_reconstruction.py (numpy and matplotlib required).
This is a transparent forward sensitivity model, NOT a fitted thermal history.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import csv, json, math, shutil, zipfile
import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt

P=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
AGES=np.array(sorted(set([round(x,3) for x in np.arange(0,4.651,.05)]+[4.685,4.567,4.54]),reverse=True))
DZ=.05
Z=np.arange(DZ/2,25,DZ) # midpoint quadrature; depth in km
# Half lives in Ga; heat rates W/kg isotope, rounded Ruedas (2017).
ISOTOPES={'U238':(4.468,9.495e-5,.9927955),'U235':(.704,5.684e-4,.0072045),
          'Th232':(14.0,2.618e-5,1.),'K40':(1.248,2.876e-5,.000117)}
# Present-day equivalent element mass fractions. Values are scenario choices.
# Mars K2O=0.5 wt% -> K=0.415 wt%, motivated by McLennan et al. (2026).
# Earth uses fixed-area continental and oceanic columns, not one global crust.
DOMAINS={
 'Mars':dict(planet='Mars',area=144.37e6,weight=1.,rho=2900,k=2.5,L=50.,K=.00415,U=.16e-6,Th=.60e-6,qb=13.,cool=.20),
 'Earth_continent':dict(planet='Earth',area=510.1e6,weight=.29,rho=2800,k=3.,L=35.,K=.015,U=1.3e-6,Th=5.6e-6,qb=28.,cool=.17),
 'Earth_ocean':dict(planet='Earth',area=510.1e6,weight=.71,rho=2900,k=2.5,L=7.,K=.0015,U=.10e-6,Th=.40e-6,qb=95.,cool=.12)}
SCENARIOS=['deep_20km','upper_5p4_8km','legacy_10km']
SOURCES=[
 ['Ruedas 2017','https://doi.org/10.1002/2017GC006997','Isotope heat rates and half-lives; rounded constants.'],
 ['McLennan et al. 2026','https://doi.org/10.1029/2025RG000882','Post-InSight crust review; K2O ~0.5 wt%; HPE distribution complex.'],
 ['Mackay-Champion et al. 2026','https://doi.org/10.1038/s41550-026-02907-5','Layering and magmatic history; excludes treating one steady geotherm as definitive.'],
 ['Sun et al. 2025','https://doi.org/10.1093/nsr/nwaf166','Alternative water layer 5.4–8 km, 20–30% porosity; conditional global extrapolation.'],
 ['Wright et al. 2024','https://doi.org/10.1073/pnas.2409983121','Alternative 11.5–20 km layer; 0.17 +/- 0.07 porosity near InSight.'],
 ['Shi et al. 2025/2026','https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-025-67784-4','Inferred seasonal shallow brines, not global fresh groundwater. Excluded from pure-water window.'],
 ['Ultra-hot origins of stable continents 2025','https://doi.org/10.1038/s41561-025-01820-2','Earth K/U/Th heat rates, time dependence and crustal stratification.'],
 ['USGS age of Earth','https://pubs.usgs.gov/gip/geotime/age.html','Earth 4.54 Ga; 4.685 Ga is pre-formation.'],
 ['NASA Solar System age','https://astrobiology.nasa.gov/news/age-of-the-solar-system-needs-to-be-recalculated/','CAI reference about 4.567 Ga.'],
 ['USGS Mars chronology','https://www.usgs.gov/publications/geologic-history-mars','Approximate Martian chronology; absolute dates uncertain.']]

def writecsv(name,rows):
    rows=list(rows)
    with (P/name).open('w',newline='') as f:
        w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)

def radiogenic(d,age):
    """Decay a conserved equivalent inventory: A(age)=sum A_now*2**(age/half_life).
    This assumes secular equilibrium U/Th chains and no element redistribution.
    Earth inventory is an endmember, NOT a claim of an unchanged primordial crust.
    """
    parts={}
    for iso,(half,h,f) in ISOTOPES.items():
        el='U' if iso.startswith('U') else 'Th' if iso.startswith('Th') else 'K'
        parts[iso]=d['rho']*d[el]*f*h*2**(age/half)
    # Extinct-nuclide diagnostic endmembers referenced to 4.567 Ga CAI time.
    # Al=8 wt%, Fe=12 wt%; initial number ratios Al26/Al27=5.2e-5,
    # Fe60/Fe56=1e-8 are assumed, not inferred primordial crust compositions.
    elapsed=4.567-age
    parts['Al26']=d['rho']*.08*(26/27)*5.2e-5*.3583*2**(-elapsed/.000717)
    parts['Fe60']=d['rho']*.12*(60/56)*1e-8*.0364*2**(-elapsed/.00262)
    return parts

def thermal(d,age,heat_scale=1):
    A=sum(radiogenic(d,age).values())*heat_scale # W/m3
    qb=d['qb']*math.exp(d['cool']*age)*heat_scale # mW/m2; assumed cooling history
    q=qb+A*d['L']*1e6 # mW/m2
    if d['planet']=='Mars': ts=-60+35*age/4.4
    elif d['weight']==.29: ts=12+8*age/4.4
    else: ts=2+8*age/4.4
    # Uniform heat production in column thickness L. Below crust: constant qb.
    a=np.minimum(Z,d['L'])*1000
    T=ts+((q/1000)*a-A*a*a/2)/d['k']+qb/d['k']*np.maximum(0,Z-d['L'])
    return T,A,q,qb,ts

def water(d,age,scenario):
    if d['planet']=='Earth':
        phi=.15*np.exp(-Z/1.5)
        phi*=np.clip((4-Z)/.5,0,1) # retains original 4 km boundary
        sat=.8
    else:
        shallow=.25*np.exp(-Z/5)
        if scenario=='deep_20km':
            blend=np.clip((Z-8)/3.5,0,1)
            phi=((1-blend)*shallow+blend*.17)*np.clip((20-Z),0,1)
            sat=.65+.25*age/4.4
        elif scenario=='upper_5p4_8km':
            # Distinct alternative, never added to deep reservoir.
            layer=np.clip((Z-5.4)/.1,0,1)*np.clip((8-Z)/.1,0,1)
            young=.25*layer
            blend=age/4.4
            phi=(1-blend)*young+blend*shallow*np.clip((10-Z),0,1)
            sat=1.
        else:
            phi=shallow*np.clip((10-Z),0,1)
            sat=.25+.65*age/4.4
    return phi*sat

def column(d,age,scenario,heat_scale=1):
    T,A,q,qb,ts=thermal(d,age,heat_scale)
    w=water(d,age,scenario)
    # Original tapered 0–80 C metric retained; plateau is 10–65 C.
    score=np.clip(T/10,0,1)*np.clip((80-T)/15,0,1)
    liquid=(T>=0)&(T<=80)
    dv=d['area']*d['weight']*DZ*w
    vh=float(np.sum(dv*score));vw=float(sum(dv));vl=float(sum(dv*liquid))
    mean=float(np.sum(dv*liquid*T)/vl) if vl else None
    mh=float(np.sum(dv*score*T)/vh) if vh else None
    return dict(T=T,A=A,q=q,qb=qb,ts=ts,w=w,score=score,dv=dv,
                vh=vh,vw=vw,vl=vl,mean=mean,mh=mh)

def planet(pl,age,scenario,scale=1):
    cs=[column(d,age,scenario,scale) for d in DOMAINS.values() if d['planet']==pl]
    vh=sum(c['vh'] for c in cs);vw=sum(c['vw'] for c in cs);vl=sum(c['vl'] for c in cs)
    weights=[d['weight'] for d in DOMAINS.values() if d['planet']==pl]
    return dict(volume=vh,water=vw,liquid=vl,fraction=vh/vw,
                mean=sum((c['mean'] or 0)*c['vl'] for c in cs)/vl if vl else None,
                hab_mean=sum((c['mh'] or 0)*c['vh'] for c in cs)/vh if vh else None,
                heat=sum(w*c['q'] for w,c in zip(weights,cs)),
                profile=np.sum([c['w']*c['score']*w for c,w in zip(cs,weights)],axis=0),
                temp=cs[0]['T'],columns=cs)

def epoch(pl,t):
    if t>4.567:return 'Pre-Solar-System'
    if t>4.4:return 'Accretion/magma-ocean: unresolved'
    if pl=='Mars':return 'Pre-Noachian' if t>4.1 else 'Noachian' if t>3.7 else 'Hesperian' if t>3 else 'Amazonian'
    return 'Hadean' if t>4 else 'Archean' if t>2.5 else 'Proterozoic' if t>.5388 else 'Phanerozoic'

def run():
    rows=[];depthrows=[];iso=[];data={}; summary={}
    for name,d in DOMAINS.items():
        for t in sorted(set(AGES.tolist()+[round(4.567-x,6) for x in np.arange(0,.10001,.0001)]),reverse=True):
            if t>4.567:continue
            parts=radiogenic(d,t)
            iso.append(dict(domain=name,age_Ga=t,**{k+'_W_m3':v for k,v in parts.items()},A_total_W_m3=sum(parts.values())))
    for sc in SCENARIOS:
        series={pl:[] for pl in ['Mars','Earth']}
        for t in AGES:
            valid=t<=4.4
            pair={pl:planet(pl,t,sc) if valid else None for pl in series}
            row=dict(scenario=sc,age_Ga=t,status='modeled_scenario' if valid else 'pre_solar_system' if t>4.567 else 'formation_unresolved',
                     mars_epoch=epoch('Mars',t),earth_epoch=epoch('Earth',t))
            for pl,r in pair.items():
                pre=pl.lower()+'_'
                for field,key in [('habitable_water_equivalent_km3','volume'),('assigned_pore_water_km3','water'),('water_0_80C_km3','liquid'),('normalized_fraction','fraction'),('liquid_water_mean_C','mean'),('suitability_weighted_mean_C','hab_mean'),('surface_heat_mW_m2','heat')]:row[pre+field]=r[key] if r else None
                series[pl].append(None if not r else {k:round(r[k],6) if r[k] is not None else None for k in ['volume','water','liquid','fraction','mean','hab_mean','heat']}|{'profile':np.round(r['profile'][::5],5).tolist(),'temp':np.round(r['temp'][::5],2).tolist()})
                if r:
                    for name,d in DOMAINS.items():
                        if d['planet']!=pl:continue
                        c=column(d,t,sc)
                        for j,z in enumerate(Z):
                            depthrows.append(dict(scenario=sc,planet=pl,domain=name,age_Ga=t,depth_km=round(z,3),temperature_C=round(c['T'][j],5),water_fraction_rock=round(c['w'][j],7),thermal_score=round(c['score'][j],7),habitable_water_cell_km3=round(c['dv'][j]*c['score'][j],4),water_cell_km3=round(c['dv'][j],4)))
            row['mars_minus_earth_habitable_water_km3']=pair['Mars']['volume']-pair['Earth']['volume'] if valid else None
            row['mars_minus_earth_normalized_percentage_points']=100*(pair['Mars']['fraction']-pair['Earth']['fraction']) if valid else None
            rows.append(row)
        valid=[i for i,t in enumerate(AGES) if t<=4.4]
        peak=max(valid,key=lambda i:series['Mars'][i]['volume'])
        threshold=.9*series['Mars'][peak]['volume']
        opt=[i for i in valid if series['Mars'][i]['volume']>=threshold]
        # Same-age similarity: RMS of fraction mismatch and liquid-temperature mismatch.
        # Temperature scaled by 80 C; comparable water-normalized conditions, not equal total volumes.
        distances={i:math.sqrt(((series['Mars'][i]['fraction']-series['Earth'][i]['fraction'])**2+((series['Mars'][i]['mean']-series['Earth'][i]['mean'])/80)**2)/2) for i in valid if series['Mars'][i]['mean'] is not None and series['Earth'][i]['mean'] is not None}
        best=min(distances.values());similar=next(i for i in valid if i in distances and distances[i]<=best+1e-9)
        summary[sc]=dict(peak_age_Ga=AGES[peak],peak_water_km3=series['Mars'][peak]['volume'],optimal_ages_Ga=AGES[opt].tolist(),first_most_similar_age_Ga=AGES[similar],similarity_RMS=distances[similar],similar_index=similar,peak_index=peak)
        data[sc]=series
    writecsv('habitability_timeseries.csv',rows)
    writecsv('geotherms_depth_time.csv',depthrows)
    writecsv('isotope_heat_production.csv',iso)
    writecsv('parameters.csv',[dict(domain=k,**v) for k,v in DOMAINS.items()])
    writecsv('sources.csv',[dict(citation=a,url=b,use=c) for a,b,c in SOURCES])
    diff=[{k:v for k,v in r.items() if k in ['scenario','age_Ga','status','mars_epoch','earth_epoch','mars_minus_earth_habitable_water_km3','mars_minus_earth_normalized_percentage_points'] or 'normalized_fraction' in k or 'habitable_water_equivalent' in k} for r in rows]
    writecsv('mars_minus_earth_timeseries.csv',diff)
    sens=[]
    for sc in SCENARIOS:
        for scale in [.7,1.,1.3]:
            for t in AGES[AGES<=4.4]:
                m=planet('Mars',t,sc,scale);e=planet('Earth',t,sc,scale)
                sens.append(dict(scenario=sc,heat_scale=scale,age_Ga=t,mars_km3=m['volume'],earth_km3=e['volume'],difference_km3=m['volume']-e['volume'],difference_pp=100*(m['fraction']-e['fraction'])))
    writecsv('heat_sensitivity.csv',sens)
    blob=dict(ages=AGES.tolist(),depths=Z[::5].tolist(),data=data,summary=summary)
    (P/'model_data.json').write_text(json.dumps(blob,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False))
    (P/'summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
    template=(P/'interactive_template.html').read_text()
    for mode,name in [('compare','mars_earth_radiogenic_water_interactive'),('difference','mars_minus_earth_radiogenic_water_interactive')]:
        fragment=template.replace('__DATA__',json.dumps(blob,separators=(',',':'))).replace('__MODE__',mode).replace('__ROOT__','radiogenic-'+mode)
        (P/(name+'_embed.html')).write_text(fragment)
        (P/(name+'.html')).write_text('<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>'+name+'</title></head><body style="margin:0;background:#091522">'+fragment+'</body></html>')
    figures(blob)
    check(rows)
    print(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))

def figures(blob):
    plt.rcParams.update({'font.size':10,'svg.fonttype':'none','font.family':'DejaVu Sans'})
    sc='deep_20km';s=blob['data'][sc];valid=[i for i,t in enumerate(AGES) if t<=4.4];t=AGES[valid]
    fig,axs=plt.subplots(3,1,figsize=(10,10),sharex=True,layout='constrained')
    for pl,col in [('Mars','#ad533d'),('Earth','#227ea8')]:
        axs[0].plot(t,[s[pl][i]['volume']/1e6 for i in valid],label=pl,color=col,lw=2)
        axs[1].plot(t,[100*s[pl][i]['fraction'] for i in valid],label=pl,color=col,lw=2)
    delta=[(s['Mars'][i]['volume']-s['Earth'][i]['volume'])/1e6 for i in valid]
    axs[2].plot(t,delta,color='#664994',lw=2);axs[2].axhline(0,color='grey',lw=.8)
    opt=blob['summary'][sc]['optimal_ages_Ga']
    for ax in axs:
        ax.axvspan(min(opt)-.025,max(opt)+.025,color='green',alpha=.12)
        ax.set_xlim(4.685,0);ax.grid(alpha=.2);ax.axvspan(4.4,4.685,color='grey',alpha=.12)
    axs[0].legend();axs[0].set_ylabel('Weighted pore-water volume\n(million km³)');axs[1].set_ylabel('Water-normalized suitability (%)');axs[2].set_ylabel('Mars − Earth\n(million km³)');axs[2].set_xlabel('Time before present (Ga)')
    fig.suptitle('Radiogenic geotherms and conditional aqueous habitat\nDeep Mars reservoir scenario; green ≥90% Martian maximum')
    fig.savefig(P/'radiogenic_comparison.svg');fig.savefig(P/'radiogenic_comparison.png',dpi=180);plt.close(fig)
    fig,axs=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(12,5),layout='constrained',sharey=True)
    for ax,pl in zip(axs,['Mars','Earth']):
        arr=np.array([s[pl][i]['profile'] for i in valid]).T
        im=ax.pcolormesh(t,blob['depths'],arr,shading='nearest',cmap='viridis',vmin=0,vmax=.17)
        ax.invert_xaxis();ax.set_ylim(25,0);ax.set_title(pl);ax.set_xlabel('Time before present (Ga)')
    axs[0].set_ylabel('Depth (km)');fig.colorbar(im,ax=axs,label='Water fraction × thermal suitability');fig.savefig(P/'depth_time.svg');plt.close(fig)

def check(rows):
    global Z,DZ
    valid=[r for r in rows if r['status']=='modeled_scenario']
    for r in valid:
        for pl in ['mars','earth']:
            assert 0<=r[pl+'_normalized_fraction']<=1
            assert r[pl+'_habitable_water_equivalent_km3']<=r[pl+'_assigned_pore_water_km3']
        assert abs(r['mars_minus_earth_habitable_water_km3']-(r['mars_habitable_water_equivalent_km3']-r['earth_habitable_water_equivalent_km3']))<1e-6
    # Heat equation: dT/dz=q(z)/k and qsurface=qb+A*L.
    d=DOMAINS['Mars'];c=column(d,0,'deep_20km');grad=np.gradient(c['T'],Z*1000)
    expected=(c['q']/1000-c['A']*Z*1000)/d['k']
    assert np.max(abs(grad[1:-1]-expected[1:-1]))<1e-10
    # Long-lived isotope contributions must decline towards the present.
    for d in DOMAINS.values():assert sum(radiogenic(d,4.4).values())>sum(radiogenic(d,0).values())
    # Midpoint resolution verification at the main scientific comparison ages.
    oldz,olddz=Z.copy(),DZ
    errors=[]
    for pl in ['Mars','Earth']:
        for t in [4.4,3.85,0]:
            base=planet(pl,t,'deep_20km')['volume']
            Z=np.arange(.0125,25,.025);DZ=.025
            fine=planet(pl,t,'deep_20km')['volume'];Z=oldz;DZ=olddz
            errors.append(abs(base-fine)/max(fine,1))
    assert max(errors)<.005
    (P/'validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(rows=len(rows),depth_spacing_km=.05,time_step_Ga=.05,max_relative_volume_quadrature_error=max(errors),checks='bounds, exact subtraction, heat-equation gradient, decay monotonicity, depth refinement passed'),indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':run()


================================================================================

"""Retrieve bibliographic metadata from the DOI registration agency."""
import urllib.request,json,time
from pathlib import Path
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
DOIS=['10.1016/j.icarus.2023.115734','10.1002/2017GC006997','10.1029/2025RG000882','10.1038/s41550-026-02907-5','10.1016/j.pepi.2025.107383','10.1029/2022JE007539','10.1029/2020GL088653','10.1111/j.1468-8123.2010.00278.x','10.5194/se-1-5-2010','10.1038/srep45629','10.1126/science.abc7717','10.1038/nature24657','10.1051/0004-6361/201526146','10.1126/sciadv.adm7499','10.1038/s41559-024-02461-1','10.1038/nmicrobiol.2016.116','10.1093/molbev/msz180','10.1089/ast.2013.1028','10.1006/icar.1999.6317','10.1038/s41561-019-0380-0','10.1038/35051550','10.1038/s41550-024-02414-5','10.1038/s41467-026-72209-x','10.1016/j.icarus.2021.114499','10.1038/s41550-020-01290-z','10.3847/PSJ/ad9b24','10.1016/j.epsl.2026.120235','10.1073/pnas.2409983121','10.1093/nsr/nwaf166','10.1038/s41550-022-01836-3','10.1038/s41550-024-02268-x']
def get(doi):
 try:
  with urllib.request.urlopen('https://api.crossref.org/works/'+doi,timeout=25) as r:return doi,json.load(r)['message']
 except Exception as e:return doi,{'error':str(e)}
with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=6) as ex:r=dict(ex.map(get,DOIS))
(P/'bibliography_metadata.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2));print('References',len(r),'failed',[(d,v) for d,v in r.items() if 'error'in v])

"""4.4 Ga aqueous-habitat scenarios with explicit water conservation.
Run from any directory: python source/build_model.py
Dependencies numpy matplotlib. Fixed seed; all epistemic choices are explicit.
"""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,csv,json,math,base64
import numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from matplotlib.patches import FancyArrowPatch
import thermal_foundation as th
P=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
AGES=np.round(np.arange(4.4,-.001,-.05),2); Z=th.Z; DZ=th.DZ
th.DOMAINS['Earth_ocean']['qb']=105.
SC={
 'conservative':dict(W0=500.,hyd=350.,escape=130.,mars_close=20.,earth_close=10.,creep=350.,heat=.85),
 'reference':dict(W0=1100.,hyd=550.,escape=100.,mars_close=22.5,earth_close=15.,creep=450.,heat=1.),
 'water_rich':dict(W0=1500.,hyd=550.,escape=20.,mars_close=25.,earth_close=20.,creep=550.,heat=1.15)}

def csvout(name,rows):
 rows=list(rows)
 temp=P/(name+'.tmp')
 with temp.open('w',newline='') as f:
  w=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));w.writeheader();w.writerows(rows)
 temp.replace(P/name)
 with (P/name).open() as f:assert sum(1 for _ in csv.DictReader(f))==len(rows), name

def extract_prior():
 src=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]/'source_downloads/tharsis_traces.json'
 if src.exists():
  d=json.loads(src.read_text());rows=[]
  labels=['Literature-scale post-4Ga','Generous Tharsis','Extreme 3-bar cumulative','Inherited 2.5-bar reservoir']
  def dec(v):return np.frombuffer(base64.b64decode(v['bdata']),dtype=v['dtype']) if isinstance(v,dict) else np.array(v)
  for k,label in enumerate(labels):
   x=dec(d[k*11+8]['x']);yy=[dec(d[k*11+j]['y']) for j in [8,9,10]]
   for i,t in enumerate(x):rows.append(dict(scenario=label,age_Ga=t,outgassed_GEL_m=yy[0][i],remaining_GEL_m=yy[1][i],escaped_GEL_m=yy[2][i],provenance='Historical project scenario extracted unchanged; not measured total inventory'))
  csvout('prior_tharsis_GEL_timeseries.csv',rows)
 elif not (P/'prior_tharsis_GEL_timeseries.csv').exists():raise FileNotFoundError('Provide prior_tharsis_GEL_timeseries.csv for historical figure')

# A cumulative integral on a fine time grid makes endmember totals exact.
FINE=np.linspace(4.4,0,4401);ELAP=4.4-FINE
# Simplified young-Sun power law, normalized today; not the fitted Tu tracks.
EUV=(4.567/np.maximum(.1,4.567-FINE))**1.23
HR=.2*np.exp(-ELAP/.18)+np.exp(-.5*((FINE-3.9)/.38)**2)
def cumulative(rate):
 y=np.r_[0,np.cumsum((rate[1:]+rate[:-1])*.5*.001)];return y/y[-1]
HY=cumulative(HR);ES=cumulative(EUV)
def frac(curve,t):return float(np.interp(4.4-t,ELAP,curve))
def budget(t,s):
 d=22*max(0,(1-np.exp(-max(0,4-t)/.8))/(1-np.exp(-4/.8)))
 b=s['hyd']*frac(HY,t);e=s['escape']*frac(ES,t);free=s['W0']+d-b-e
 assert free>=-1e-8
 return dict(initial=s['W0'],outgas=d,bound=b,escaped=e,free=free,euv=float(np.interp(4.4-t,ELAP,EUV)))

def column(name,t,s):
 d=th.DOMAINS[name];T,A,q,qb,ts=th.thermal(d,t,s['heat']);zm=Z*1000
 gradient=((q/1000)-A*np.minimum(zm,d['L']*1000))/d['k']*1000
 if d['planet']=='Mars':
  # Fractured basalt endmember, depth distribution uncertain beyond local paths.
  blend=np.clip((Z-8)/3.5,0,1);phi=(1-blend)*(.25*np.exp(-Z/5))+blend*.12
  visc=np.clip((s['creep']-T)/100,0,1)
  phi*=np.clip((s['mars_close']-Z)/1.,0,1)*visc
  b=budget(t,s)
  # Allocate mineral-bound water across depth, not as liquid. 1 wt% global
  # crustal-water capacity is an explicit endmember, not a measured clay amount.
  depthweight=np.exp(-Z/8)
  # Retained clay hydration records earlier alteration; current cold temperatures
  # do not release structurally bound water. Allocate with a local 1 wt% H2O cap.
  cap=np.full_like(Z,.01*d['rho']/1000*DZ) # water km per depth bin
  bound_km=np.zeros_like(Z);remaining=b['bound']/1000
  for _ in range(100):
   available=cap-bound_km;weights=depthweight*(available>1e-14)
   if remaining<1e-13 or weights.sum()==0:break
   add=np.minimum(available,remaining*weights/weights.sum())
   bound_km+=add;remaining-=float(add.sum())
  assert remaining<1e-8, 'Hydration capacity exceeded'
  occlusion=.5*bound_km/DZ
  phi=np.maximum(0,phi-occlusion)
  capacity=float(np.sum(phi)*DZ*1000)
  assigned=min(.8*b['free'],capacity) # GEL m; rest surface ice/vapor/deep/unallocated
  sat=assigned/capacity if capacity else 0.
  water=phi*sat
  closure=float(Z[np.where(phi>1e-5)[0][-1]]) if np.any(phi>1e-5) else 0.
 else:
  # No universal Earth closure: an effective connected-domain endmember,
  # capped by modeled brittle/ductile temperature and a depth range.
  length=1.5 if name=='Earth_continent' else 1.1
  phi=(.15 if name=='Earth_continent' else .10)*np.exp(-Z/length)
  maxdepth=min(s['earth_close'],d['L'])
  phi*=np.clip((maxdepth-Z),0,1)*np.clip((350-T)/100,0,1)
  water=.8*phi;assigned=None;capacity=None;bound_km=np.zeros_like(Z)
  closure=float(Z[np.where(phi>1e-5)[0][-1]]) if np.any(phi>1e-5) else 0.
 score=np.clip(T/10,0,1)*np.clip((80-T)/15,0,1)
 dv=d['area']*d['weight']*DZ*water;vl=float(sum(dv*((T>=0)&(T<=80))))
 vh=float(sum(dv*score));vw=float(sum(dv));mask=(T>=0)&(T<=80)
 return dict(temp=T,profile=water*score,water_profile=water,phi=phi,score=score,cell_water=dv,volume=vh,water=vw,liquid=vl,mean=float(sum(dv*mask*T)/vl) if vl else None,hab_mean=float(sum(dv*score*T)/vh) if vh else None,heat=q,gradient=gradient,closure=closure,bound_cells_GEL=bound_km*1000,A=A,qb=qb,ts=ts)

def planet(pl,t,s):
 cols=[(name,column(name,t,s)) for name,d in th.DOMAINS.items() if d['planet']==pl];weights=[th.DOMAINS[n]['weight'] for n,c in cols]
 v=sum(c['volume'] for n,c in cols);w=sum(c['water'] for n,c in cols);vl=sum(c['liquid'] for n,c in cols)
 return dict(volume=v,water=w,liquid=vl,fraction=v/w if w else 0,mean=sum((c['mean'] or 0)*c['liquid'] for n,c in cols)/vl if vl else None,hab_mean=sum((c['hab_mean'] or 0)*c['volume'] for n,c in cols)/v if v else None,heat=sum(c['heat']*a for (n,c),a in zip(cols,weights)),gradient=sum(float(c['gradient'][0])*a for (n,c),a in zip(cols,weights)),closure=sum(c['closure']*a for (n,c),a in zip(cols,weights)),profile=np.sum([c['profile']*a for (n,c),a in zip(cols,weights)],axis=0),columns=cols)

def run():
 extract_prior();rows=[];depth=[];bud=[];D={};summary={}
 for sc,s in SC.items():
  D[sc]={'Mars':[],'Earth':[]}
  for t in AGES:
   b=budget(t,s);bud.append(dict(scenario=sc,age_Ga=t,**{k+'_GEL_m':v for k,v in b.items() if k!='euv'},EUV_relative_today=b['euv'],mass_balance_residual_GEL_m=b['initial']+b['outgas']-b['bound']-b['escaped']-b['free']))
   pair={pl:planet(pl,t,s) for pl in ['Mars','Earth']};r=dict(scenario=sc,age_Ga=t)
   for pl,c in pair.items():
    D[sc][pl].append({k:round(float(c[k]),6) if c[k] is not None else None for k in ['volume','water','liquid','fraction','mean','hab_mean','heat','gradient','closure']}|{'profile':np.round(c['profile'][::5],6).tolist()})
    for k in ['volume','water','liquid','fraction','mean','hab_mean','heat','gradient','closure']:r[pl.lower()+'_'+k]=c[k]
    for name,col in c['columns']:
     for j,z in enumerate(Z):depth.append(dict(scenario=sc,domain=name,age_Ga=t,depth_km=round(z,3),temperature_C=col['temp'][j],gradient_C_km=col['gradient'][j],porosity_after_occlusion=col['phi'][j],assigned_water_rock_fraction=col['water_profile'][j],thermal_score=col['score'][j],habitat_cell_km3=col['cell_water'][j]*col['score'][j],bound_water_cell_GEL_m=col['bound_cells_GEL'][j]))
   r['mars_minus_earth_km3']=pair['Mars']['volume']-pair['Earth']['volume'];r['mars_minus_earth_pp']=100*(pair['Mars']['fraction']-pair['Earth']['fraction']);rows.append(r)
   assert pair['Mars']['water']/144.37e6*1000<=b['free']+1e-6
  m=D[sc]['Mars'];e=D[sc]['Earth'];peak=max(range(len(AGES)),key=lambda i:m[i]['volume']);opt=[float(AGES[i]) for i in range(len(AGES)) if m[i]['volume']>=.9*m[peak]['volume']]
  dist=[math.sqrt(((a['fraction']-b['fraction'])**2+(((a['mean'] or 0)-(b['mean'] or 0))/80)**2)/2) for a,b in zip(m,e)];similar=int(np.argmin(dist))
  summary[sc]=dict(peak_age_Ga=float(AGES[peak]),peak_water_km3=m[peak]['volume'],optimal_ages_Ga=opt,first_most_similar_age_Ga=float(AGES[similar]),similar_index=similar,peak_index=peak,similarity_RMS=dist[similar])
 csvout('habitability_44_timeseries.csv',rows);csvout('geotherms_44_depth_time.csv',depth);csvout('mars_GEL_mass_balance.csv',bud)
 csvout('mars_minus_earth_44.csv',[{k:r[k] for k in ['scenario','age_Ga','mars_volume','earth_volume','mars_fraction','earth_fraction','mars_minus_earth_km3','mars_minus_earth_pp']} for r in rows])
 csvout('scenario_parameters.csv',[dict(scenario=k,**v) for k,v in SC.items()])
 # Six isotopes inherited and explicitly exported over the entire 4.4 Ga interval.
 iso=[]
 for name,d in th.DOMAINS.items():
  for t in AGES:iso.append(dict(domain=name,age_Ga=t,**th.radiogenic(d,t)))
 csvout('isotope_heat_W_m3.csv',iso)
 luca=[]
 for sc,s in SC.items():
  for t in [4.33,4.2,4.09]:
   for pl in ['Mars','Earth']:
    c=planet(pl,t,s);luca.append(dict(scenario=sc,age_Ga=t,planet=pl,mean_0_80C_water_C=c['mean'],suitability_weighted_mean_C=c['hab_mean'],habitat_water_km3=c['volume'],normalized_fraction=c['fraction']))
 csvout('LUCA_temperatures.csv',luca)
 blob=dict(ages=AGES.tolist(),depths=Z[::5].tolist(),data=D,summary=summary,budgets={sc:[b for b in bud if b['scenario']==sc] for sc in SC},LUCA=dict(age=4.2,old=4.33,young=4.09))
 (P/'model_data_44.json').write_text(json.dumps(blob,separators=(',',':'),allow_nan=False));(P/'summary_44.json').write_text(json.dumps(summary,indent=2))
 bayes=bayesian();plots(blob,bayes);validate(rows,bud)
 print('LUCA reference', [r for r in luca if r['scenario']=='reference' and r['age_Ga']==4.2]);print('maxima',summary);print('Bayesian',bayes)

def bayesian(n=60000):
 rng=np.random.default_rng(20261031)
 # Posterior over simulation outcomes only, not observations of living transfers.
 p=rng.dirichlet(np.array([1048,617,6362,40500-1048-617-6362])+.5,n)
 reverse=rng.beta(79.5,43500-79+.5,n)
 N=10**rng.uniform(3,9,n);inoc=10**rng.uniform(-6,-.3,n);eject=10**rng.uniform(-5,0,n)
 tau=10**rng.uniform(-2,1,n) # Myr survival scale; uncalibrated ancient consortia
 establish=10**rng.uniform(-8,-1,n)
 # Simplified delivery times anchored to cited medians, not an N-body rerun.
 te=rng.lognormal(np.log(5.2),.8,n);tv=rng.lognormal(np.log(5.8),.8,n)
 onset_m=rng.uniform(4.2,4.5,n);onset_e=rng.uniform(4.1,4.4,n);clock=np.clip(rng.normal(4.2,.061),4.09,4.33)
 window_e=(onset_m-te/1000>=clock)&(onset_e>=clock)
 window_v=(onset_m-tv/1000>=clock)
 base=N*inoc*eject*establish
 le=base*p[:,0]*np.exp(-te/tau)*window_e;lv=base*p[:,1]*np.exp(-tv/tau)*window_v
 pe=-np.expm1(-le);pv=-np.expm1(-lv)
 # Habitability timing is uncertain, so this is a prior-predictive origin model.
 # L_M conditions on Mars inhabited, L_I on a specified abiogenesis prior.
 origin=[]
 for low in [-9,-6,-3]:
  q=10**rng.uniform(low,0,n);qm=10**rng.uniform(low,0,n);LM=float(np.mean(qm*pe));LI=float(np.mean(q*(onset_e>=clock)))
  bf=LM/LI if LI else None
  for prior in [.01,.1,.5]:origin.append(dict(independent_abiogenesis_log10_lower=low,prior_mars_origin=prior,prior_predictive_L_M=LM,prior_predictive_L_E=LI,bayes_factor_assumed_model=bf,posterior_mars_origin=prior*LM/(prior*LM+(1-prior)*LI)))
 csvout('bayesian_origin_prior_sensitivity.csv',origin)
 csvout('bayesian_feasibility_samples.csv',[dict(draw=i,p_transfer_Earth=p[i,0],p_transfer_Venus=p[i,1],p_reverse_Earth_to_Mars=reverse[i],candidate_rocks=N[i],inoculated_fraction=inoc[i],ejection_survival=eject[i],survival_scale_Myr=tau[i],establishment_probability=establish[i],Earth_delivery_Myr=te[i],Venus_delivery_Myr=tv[i],p_seed_Earth_given_Mars_life=pe[i],p_seed_Venus_given_Mars_life=pv[i]) for i in range(n)])
 out=dict(draws=n,transfer_Earth_quantiles=np.quantile(p[:,0],[.025,.5,.975]).tolist(),transfer_Venus_quantiles=np.quantile(p[:,1],[.025,.5,.975]).tolist(),inward_outward_ratio_median=float(np.median(p[:,0]/reverse)),Earth_seed_probability_mean=float(pe.mean()),Earth_seed_probability_quantiles=np.quantile(pe,[.025,.5,.975]).tolist(),Venus_seed_probability_mean=float(pv.mean()),Venus_seed_probability_quantiles=np.quantile(pv,[.025,.5,.975]).tolist(),interpretation='Simulation-count posterior plus explicitly assumed biological priors; not empirical probability of Martian origin')
 (P/'bayesian_summary.json').write_text(json.dumps(out,indent=2));return out

def save(fig,name):
 fig.savefig(P/(name+'.svg'));fig.savefig(P/(name+'.png'),dpi=220);plt.close(fig)

def plots(blob,bayes):
 plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':12,'svg.fonttype':'none','axes.spines.top':False,'axes.spines.right':False})
 D=blob['data'];ref=D['reference'];m=ref['Mars'];e=ref['Earth'];opt=blob['summary']['reference']['optimal_ages_Ga']
 def decorate(ax):
  ax.set_xlim(4.4,0);ax.axvspan(4.09,4.33,color='.55',alpha=.16);ax.axvline(4.2,color='.45',ls=':',lw=1);ax.grid(alpha=.18)

 fig.colorbar(im,ax=axs[0],label='Water fraction × thermal suitability',shrink=.8)

 for ax in axs[1]:decorate(ax);ax.axvspan(min(opt)-.025,max(opt)+.025,color='#2caa50',alpha=.13);ax.set_xlabel('Time before present (Ga)')
 axs[1,0].set_ylabel('Suitable water equivalent\n(million km³)');axs[1,1].set_ylabel('Water normalized suitability (%)');axs[1,0].legend();fig.suptitle('Aqueous habitat scenarios from 4.4 Ga\nGrey: LUCA clock interval · Green: Mars at ≥90% of reference maximum');save(fig,'Figure_1_habitat')

def validate(rows,bud):
 err=max(abs(b['mass_balance_residual_GEL_m']) for b in bud)
 assert err<1e-9
 for r in rows:
  for pl in ['mars','earth']:
   assert 0<=r[pl+'_fraction']<=1;assert 0<=r[pl+'_volume']<=r[pl+'_water']+1e-6
  assert abs(r['mars_minus_earth_km3']-r['mars_volume']+r['earth_volume'])<1e-6
 # All lithopanspermia outcomes must stay within probability bounds.
 # Refine depth quadrature while holding budget histories fixed.
 global Z,DZ
 oz,od=Z.copy(),DZ;errors=[]
 for t in [4.4,4.2,3.85,0]:
  for pl in ['Mars','Earth']:
   a=planet(pl,t,SC['reference'])['volume'];Z=np.arange(.0125,25,.025);DZ=.025;th.Z=Z;th.DZ=DZ
   b=planet(pl,t,SC['reference'])['volume'];errors.append(abs(a-b)/max(b,1));Z=oz;DZ=od;th.Z=Z;th.DZ=DZ
 assert max(errors)<.01
 (P/'validation_44.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(time_frames=len(AGES),step_Myr=50,depth_step_km=.05,max_mass_balance_error_GEL_m=err,max_relative_depth_refinement_error=max(errors),checks='conservation, water inventory cap, suitability bounds, signed subtraction and depth refinement passed'),indent=2))

if __name__=='__main__':run()

from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd,json,shutil
from scipy.special import logsumexp
from scipy.stats import linregress
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];O=B/'work_v5/results';O.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
F=next((B/'inputs_v5/fresh').iterdir())
raw=pd.read_csv(F/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')
full=raw[['diameter_bin_km','elevation_km','fresh_depth_km','fresh_sd_km']].copy()
for c,mu,sd in [('2-3',.23662,.082141),('3-4',.38795,.10681),('4-5',.49151,.13754)]:
 full.loc[len(full)]=[c,4.5,mu,sd]
full=full.sort_values(['diameter_bin_km','elevation_km'])
full.to_csv(O/'fresh_relief_published_27_means.csv',index=False)
# Instrument the preserved entry surrogate to retain all sampled predictions and diagnostics.
source=(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py').read_text()
source=source[:source.index("res=pd.DataFrame(records)")]
source=source.replace("ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent","ROOT=Path(DATA_ROOT)")
source=source.replace("raw=pd.read_csv(ROOT/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')","raw=DATA.copy()")
source=source.replace('records=[]; per_bin=[]','records=[]; per_bin=[]; predictions=[]; losses=[]')
source=source.replace('evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)','evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)\n    predictions.append(initial.copy());losses.append(-2*lf.copy())')
def r2(y,p):
 return 1-np.sum((y-p)**2)/np.sum((y-y.mean())**2)
def within_r2(y,p):
 return 1-np.sum((y-p)**2)/np.sum((y-y.mean(axis=1)[:,None])**2)
summaries=[]
for label,data in [('archive24',raw),('full27',full)]:
 env={'__name__':'reproduce','__file__':str(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py'),'DATA_ROOT':str(F),'DATA':data}
 exec(compile(source,'archived_surrogate','exec'),env)
 records=pd.DataFrame(env['records']);pred=np.array(env['predictions']);chi=np.array(env['losses']);obs=env['fresh'];z=env['z'];sd=env['fsd']
 records['posterior_weight']=np.exp(records.log_evidence-logsumexp(records.log_evidence))
 metrics=[]
 for i,rec in records.iterrows():
  k=np.argmin(chi[i]);p=pred[i,k]
  row=rec.to_dict();row.update(sample_id=int(k),profile_chi2=float(chi[i,k]),R2_pooled=float(r2(obs,p)),R2_within_size=float(within_r2(obs,p)))
  for j,c in enumerate(env['classes']):row['R2_'+c]=r2(obs[j],p[j])
  metrics.append(row)
 metrics=pd.DataFrame(metrics);metrics.to_csv(O/f'{label}_all_240_scenario_fits.csv',index=False)
 rows=[];pc=[]
 for pressure,part in metrics.groupby('pressure_bar'):
  best=part.loc[part.profile_chi2.idxmin()];i=best.name;k=int(best.sample_id)
  p=pred[i,k];weights=np.exp(-.5*chi[i]-logsumexp(-.5*chi[i]))
  mean=np.nansum(pred[i]*weights[:,None,None],axis=0)
  std=np.sqrt(np.nansum(weights[:,None,None]*(pred[i]-mean[None,:,:])**2,axis=0))
  row=best.to_dict();row['pressure_weight']=part.posterior_weight.sum();rows.append(row)
  for j,c in enumerate(env['classes']):
   for t,alt in enumerate(z):
    pc.append(dict(pressure_bar=pressure,diameter_bin_km=c,elevation_km=alt,observed_relief_km=obs[j,t],observed_SD_km=sd[j,t],assumed_likelihood_sigma_km=env['SF'][j,t],profile_prediction_km=p[j,t],conditional_mean_km=mean[j,t],conditional_SD_km=std[j,t]))
 pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(O/f'{label}_pressure_fit_summary.csv',index=False)
 pd.DataFrame(pc).to_csv(O/f'{label}_profile_curves_and_uncertainty.csv',index=False)
 np.savez_compressed(O/f'{label}_all_sampled_predictions.npz',prediction=pred,chi2=chi,observed=obs,sd=sd,elevation=z)
 if label=='archive24':
  prior=pd.read_csv(F/'mars_fresh_only_posterior_pressure_bar.csv')
  assert np.allclose(pd.DataFrame(rows).pressure_weight,prior.probability,rtol=1e-10)
 summaries.append({'dataset':label,'pressure_results':pd.DataFrame(rows).to_dict(orient='records')})
# Ordinary empirical elevation regressions, full27 and historical24 separately.
reg=[]
for label,data in [('archive24',raw),('full27',full)]:
 for c,part in data.groupby('diameter_bin_km'):
  a=linregress(part.elevation_km,part.fresh_depth_km)
  reg.append(dict(dataset=label,diameter_bin_km=c,n_elevation_bins=len(part),slope_m_per_km=a.slope*1000,slope_standard_error_m_per_km=a.stderr*1000,intercept_km=a.intercept,R2=a.rvalue**2,pvalue=a.pvalue))
pd.DataFrame(reg).to_csv(O/'empirical_relief_elevation_regressions.csv',index=False)
# Actual crater diameter data from the deduplicated catalogue, kept separate from Table 4 relief.
cat=pd.read_csv(B/'inputs_v5/craters/fresh_craters_clean.csv',low_memory=False)
cat=cat[cat.in_scope_2_5km.eq(True)&cat.diameter_bin.notna()].copy()
stats=cat.groupby(['diameter_bin','file_bin_mid_km']).agg(n=('DIAM_CIRCL','size'),mean_diameter_km=('DIAM_CIRCL','mean'),SD_diameter_km=('DIAM_CIRCL','std')).reset_index()
stats['SE_diameter_km']=stats.SD_diameter_km/np.sqrt(stats.n)
stats.to_csv(O/'catalogue_fresh_diameter_by_elevation.csv',index=False)
cat.to_csv(O/'catalogue_fresh_analysis_rows.csv',index=False)
# Transparent single-body drag plus ablation sensitivity. Not a calibrated pressure retrieval.
# Constant density, straight trajectory, no gravity, no fragmentation, isothermal column.
def entry(d0,v0,rhoi,angle,column,Ch=.01,Q=8e6,Cd=1.,steps=400):
 d=np.full_like(column,float(d0));v=np.full_like(column,float(v0));s=np.sin(np.deg2rad(angle));dx=column/steps
 for _ in range(steps):
  dd=-Ch*v*v/(4*rhoi*Q*s)*dx
  dv=-3*Cd*v/(4*rhoi*np.maximum(d,.01)*s)*dx
  d=np.maximum(d+dd,.01);v=np.maximum(v+dv,0)
 return d,v
rows=[]
for p in [.5,1,1.5,2,3]:
 for h in [0,.03,.10]:
  for rh in [.35,.75]:
   T=270.;e=611.21*np.exp((18.678-(T-273.15)/234.5)*(T-273.15)/(257.14+T-273.15))
   water=min(.015,rh*e/(p*1e5));co2=1-h-water
   # Mole fractions require gas constant R_u / mean molar mass.
   R=8.314462618/(co2*.04401+h*.002016+water*.01801528);H=R*T/3.71
   z=np.arange(-3.5,4.51,.25);column=p*1e5*np.exp(-z*1000/H)/3.71
   for d0 in [150,250,350,450]:
    for Ch in [0,.005,.01,.03]:
     d,v=entry(d0,12000,3400,45,column,Ch=Ch)
     crater=1.3*1.16*(3400/3000)**(1/3)*d**.78*(v*v/3.71)**.22/1000
     for j,alt in enumerate(z):
      rows.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=h*100,h2o_pct=water*100,co2_pct=co2*100,relative_humidity=rh,initial_projectile_m=d0,elevation_km=alt,heat_transfer_coefficient=Ch,ablation_energy_J_kg=8e6,initial_speed_m_s=12000,entry_angle_deg=45,projectile_density_kg_m3=3400,remaining_diameter_m=d[j],ground_speed_m_s=v[j],mass_fraction=(d[j]/d0)**3,predicted_final_crater_km=crater[j],scale_height_km=H/1000))
entrydf=pd.DataFrame(rows);entrydf.to_csv(O/'drag_ablation_150_250_350_450m_sensitivity.csv',index=False)
# convergence test for Euler integration of column mass
x=np.array([3e5]);a=entry(150,12000,3400,45,x,steps=400);b=entry(150,12000,3400,45,x,steps=800)
assert abs(a[0][0]-b[0][0])/b[0][0]<.003
assert abs(a[1][0]-b[1][0])/b[1][0]<.003

colors={.5:'#8f6b49',1.:'#d39342',1.5:'#8b8b8b',2.:'#297e9a',3.:'#5c427b'}

 for ext in ['svg','pdf','png']:fig.savefig(O/f'{name}.{ext}',dpi=300,bbox_inches='tight')
 plt.close(fig)
curves=pd.read_csv(O/'full27_profile_curves_and_uncertainty.csv')

from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];O=B/'work_v5/results';F=next((B/'inputs_v5/fresh').iterdir())
source=(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py').read_text().split('res=pd.DataFrame(records)')[0]
source=source.replace("ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent","ROOT=Path(DATA_ROOT)")
source=source.replace('records=[]; per_bin=[]','records=[]; per_bin=[]; diameters=[]; losses=[]')
source=source.replace('initial=np.full((N,3,len(z)),np.nan);','initial=np.full((N,3,len(z)),np.nan); dp=np.full_like(initial,np.nan);')
source=source.replace('initial[:,j,k]=relief_ratio','dp[:,j,k]=dmean\n      initial[:,j,k]=relief_ratio')
source=source.replace('evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)','evidence=logsumexp(lf)-np.log(N)\n    diameters.append(dp.copy());losses.append(-2*lf.copy())')
env={'__file__':str(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py'),'DATA_ROOT':str(F)}
exec(source,env)
dp=np.array(env['diameters']);chi=np.array(env['losses']);records=pd.DataFrame(env['records'])
stats=pd.read_csv(O/'catalogue_fresh_diameter_by_elevation.csv')
metrics=pd.read_csv(O/'archive24_all_240_scenario_fits.csv')
rows=[];curves=[]
for i,r in records.iterrows():
 k=np.argmin(chi[i]);row=r.to_dict();rr=[]
 for j,c in enumerate(['2-3','3-4','4-5']):
  q=stats[stats.diameter_bin.eq(c)].set_index('file_bin_mid_km').reindex(env['z'])
  obs=q.mean_diameter_km.to_numpy();pred=dp[i,k,j]
  val=1-np.sum((obs-pred)**2)/np.sum((obs-obs.mean())**2);row['R2_diameter_'+c]=val;rr.append(val)
 row['note']='Prediction sampled using relief likelihood, evaluated separately against catalogue diameter means; no new diameter fitting.'
 rows.append(row)
pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(O/'actual_diameter_all_240_scenario_R2.csv',index=False)
for p,part in metrics.groupby('pressure_bar'):
 r=part.loc[part.profile_chi2.idxmin()];i=r.name
 w=np.exp(-.5*chi[i]-logsumexp(-.5*chi[i]));mu=np.sum(dp[i]*w[:,None,None],axis=0);sd=np.sqrt(np.sum(w[:,None,None]*(dp[i]-mu)**2,axis=0))
 for j,c in enumerate(['2-3','3-4','4-5']):
  for t,z in enumerate(env['z']):curves.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,diameter_bin=c,elevation_km=z,mean_diameter_km=mu[j,t],SD_diameter_km=sd[j,t]))
curves=pd.DataFrame(curves);curves.to_csv(O/'actual_diameter_model_curves.csv',index=False)
plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':14,'axes.spines.top':False,'axes.spines.right':False,'svg.fonttype':'none','pdf.fonttype':42})
colors={.5:'#8f6b49',1.:'#d39342',1.5:'#8b8b8b',2.:'#297e9a',3.:'#5c427b'}
def save(fig,name):
 for ext in ['svg','pdf','png']:fig.savefig(O/f'{name}.{ext}',dpi=300,bbox_inches='tight')
 plt.close(fig)

 for p in colors:

fig.suptitle('Actual crater diameters versus entry-surrogate predictions\nObservational SD bars; 3 bar conditional model spread shaded',y=1.04,fontsize=17)
fig.subplots_adjust(bottom=.28,wspace=.12);save(fig,'F3_Actual_Diameter_Elevation')
# Chemistry and pressure retain all marginalization over material analog and humidity.

  for j,c in enumerate(['2-3','3-4','4-5']):
   f.loc[i,'correlation_R2_'+c]=np.corrcoef(obs[j],pr[j])[0,1]**2
   off=(obs[j]-pr[j]).mean()
   f.loc[i,'offset_adjusted_R2_'+c]=1-np.sum((obs[j]-pr[j]-off)**2)/np.sum((obs[j]-obs[j].mean())**2)

from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd
from scipy.special import logsumexp
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];O=B/'work_v5/results';F=next((B/'inputs_v5/fresh').iterdir())
code=(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py').read_text().split("res=pd.DataFrame(records)")[0]
code=code.replace("ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent","ROOT=Path(DATA_ROOT)")
code=code.replace("raw=pd.read_csv(ROOT/'mars_crater_elevation_data.csv')","raw=pd.read_csv(Path(OUTPUT)/'fresh_relief_published_27_means.csv')")
code=code.replace('R=co2*188.9+h*4124+water*461.5','R=8.314462618/(co2*.04401+h*.002016+water*.01801528)')
rows=[]
for name,c in [('correct_molar_nominal',code),('correct_molar_SD_likelihood',code.replace('SF=np.sqrt((fsd/np.sqrt(20))**2+.02**2)','SF=np.sqrt(fsd**2+.02**2)'))]:
 env={'__file__':str(F/'mars_fresh_only_inversion.py'),'DATA_ROOT':str(F),'OUTPUT':str(O)}
 exec(c,env)
 t=pd.DataFrame(env['records']);t['weight']=np.exp(t.log_evidence-logsumexp(t.log_evidence))
 t.to_csv(O/(name+'_all_scenarios.csv'),index=False)

research/crater_atmosphere/Fresh_Crater_Atmosphere_and_Impact_Revision/code/ablation_fit.py
================================================================================
from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np,pandas as pd
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];O=B/'work_v5/results'
data=pd.read_csv(O/'fresh_relief_published_27_means.csv')
cat=pd.read_csv(O/'catalogue_fresh_diameter_by_elevation.csv')
D=np.geomspace(35,1400,100)[None,:,None];V=np.array([7000,10000,13000,17000,22000])[None,None,:]
w=(D/100)**-2.5*np.array([.12,.25,.29,.22,.12])[None,None,:]
z=np.arange(-3.5,4.51,1);rhoi=3400;sin=np.sin(np.pi/4)
rows=[];curves=[]
def r2(a,b):
 a=np.array(a);b=np.array(b);m=np.isfinite(a)&np.isfinite(b)
 return 1-np.sum((a[m]-b[m])**2)/np.sum((a[m]-a[m].mean())**2)
for p in [.5,1,1.5,2,3]:
 for h in [0,.03,.1]:
  for rh in [.35,.75]:
   T=270;tc=T-273.15;e=611.21*np.exp((18.678-tc/234.5)*(tc/(257.14+tc)))
   water=min(.015,rh*e/(p*1e5));R=8.314462618/((1-h-water)*.04401+h*.002016+water*.01801528)
   col=p*1e5*np.exp(-z*1000/(R*T/3.71))/3.71
   for Ch in [0,.005,.01,.03]:
    d=np.broadcast_to(D,(len(z),100,5)).copy();v=np.broadcast_to(V,d.shape).astype(float).copy();dx=col[:,None,None]/250
    for _ in range(250):
     dd=-Ch*v*v/(4*rhoi*8e6*sin)*dx;dv=-3*v/(4*rhoi*np.maximum(d,.01)*sin)*dx
     d=np.maximum(.01,d+dd);v=np.maximum(0,v+dv)
    final=1.3*1.16*(rhoi/3000)**(1/3)*d**.78*(v*v/3.71)**.22/1000
    for c,lo in [('2-3',2),('3-4',3),('4-5',4)]:
     ww=w*((final>=lo)&(final<lo+1));s=ww.sum(axis=(1,2))
     mu=(ww*final).sum(axis=(1,2))/s
     relief=(ww*.105*final*(v/12000)**.15).sum(axis=(1,2))/s
     q=cat[cat.diameter_bin.eq(c)].set_index('file_bin_mid_km').reindex(z)
     obs=data[data.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)].set_index('elevation_km').reindex(z)
     rows.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,relative_humidity=rh,heat_transfer_coefficient=Ch,diameter_bin_km=c,matched_diameter_bins=int(q.mean_diameter_km.notna().sum()),R2_diameter=r2(q.mean_diameter_km,mu),R2_relief=r2(obs.fresh_depth_km,relief),note='Uncalibrated single-body drag-ablation sensitivity with fixed stony material, no fragmentation; not evidence or a posterior'))
     for j,alt in enumerate(z):curves.append(dict(pressure_bar=p,h2_pct=100*h,relative_humidity=rh,heat_transfer_coefficient=Ch,diameter_bin_km=c,elevation_km=alt,mean_final_diameter_km=mu[j],mean_relief_km=relief[j]))
pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(O/'drag_ablation_all_360_bin_fits.csv',index=False)
pd.DataFrame(curves).to_csv(O/'drag_ablation_population_curves.csv',index=False)
print('wrote',len(rows),'ablation bin fits')

from atomic_io import install

from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd,numpy as np
import matplotlib
matplotlib.use('Agg')

O=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'work_v5/results'
plt.rcParams.update({'font.family':'DejaVu Sans','font.size':14,'axes.spines.top':False,'axes.spines.right':False,'svg.fonttype':'none','pdf.fonttype':42})
colors={.5:'#8f6b49',1.:'#d39342',1.5:'#8b8b8b',2.:'#297e9a',3.:'#5c427b'}
def save(fig,n):
 for e in ['svg','pdf','png']:fig.savefig(O/f'{n}.{e}',dpi=300,bbox_inches='tight')
 plt.close(fig)
t=pd.read_csv(O/'full27_profile_curves_and_uncertainty.csv')
fig,axs=plt.subplots(1,3,figsize=(11.3,5.4),sharex=True,sharey=True)
for ax,c in zip(axs,['2-3','3-4','4-5']):
 q=t[t.pressure_bar.eq(.5)&t.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)]
 ax.errorbar(q.elevation_km,q.observed_relief_km,yerr=q.observed_SD_km,fmt='o',color='#1b2533',ms=4,capsize=3,lw=1,label='Published mean ± 1 SD',zorder=5)
 for p,col in colors.items():
  q=t[t.pressure_bar.eq(p)&t.diameter_bin_km.eq(c)]
  ax.plot(q.elevation_km,q.conditional_mean_km,color=col,lw=2.6 if p==3 else 1.4,ls='-' if p==3 else ':',label=f'{p:g} bar')
  ax.fill_between(q.elevation_km,q.conditional_mean_km-q.conditional_SD_km,q.conditional_mean_km+q.conditional_SD_km,color=col,alpha=.065)
 ax.set(title=f'{c} km diameter',xlabel='Elevation (km)',ylim=(0,.68));ax.grid(alpha=.16)
axs[0].set_ylabel('Fresh crater relief (km)')
hs,ls=axs[0].get_legend_handles_labels();fig.legend(hs,ls,loc='lower center',ncol=3,frameon=False,bbox_to_anchor=(.5,-.03))
fig.suptitle('Fresh crater relief and the archived entry surrogate\n27 published means; conditional model means ± 1 SD; common vertical scale',y=1.04,fontsize=17)
fig.subplots_adjust(bottom=.28,wspace=.12);save(fig,'F1_Fresh_Relief_Atmospheric_Fits')
s=pd.read_csv(O/'pressure_sensitivity_gas_law_and_error_model.csv')
old=pd.read_csv(O/'archive24_pressure_fit_summary.csv');full=pd.read_csv(O/'full27_pressure_fit_summary.csv')
fig,axs=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(11.3,5.1))
for c,col in zip(['2-3','3-4','4-5'],['#297e9a','#d39342','#5c427b']):
 axs[0].plot(full.pressure_bar,full['R2_'+c],marker='o',color=col,label=f'{c} km')
axs[0].set(xlabel='Reference pressure (bar)',ylabel='Predictive R² within diameter group',title='Profile predictions against\n elevation means')
axs[0].axhline(0,color='gray',lw=.6);axs[0].grid(alpha=.16);axs[0].legend(frameon=False)
series=[(old.pressure_weight,'Original 24 means','#297e9a'),(full.pressure_weight,'Full 27 means','#5c427b'),(s[s.variant.eq('correct_molar_nominal')].weight,'Corrected gas law','#d39342'),(s[s.variant.eq('correct_molar_SD_likelihood')].weight,'SD error sensitivity','#8b8b8b')]
for j,(v,label,col) in enumerate(series):axs[1].bar(np.arange(5)+(j-1.5)*.19,np.array(v),width=.19,label=label,color=col)
axs[1].set(xticks=np.arange(5),xticklabels=['0.5','1','1.5','2','3'],xlabel='Reference pressure (bar)',ylabel='Conditional pressure-grid weight',title='Sensitivity to bin and\n uncertainty assumptions');axs[1].legend(frameon=False,fontsize=11)
fig.tight_layout();save(fig,'F2_RSquared_and_Pressure_Weights')
# Add parameter envelopes, not invented stochastic errors, to deterministic entry curves.
t=pd.read_csv(O/'drag_ablation_150_250_350_450m_sensitivity.csv')
fig,axs=plt.subplots(1,2,figsize=(10.5,4.8))
for p,col in colors.items():
 q=t[t.pressure_bar.eq(p)&t.h2_pct.eq(3)&t.relative_humidity.eq(.35)&t.initial_projectile_m.eq(250)]
 for ax,var in zip(axs,['remaining_diameter_m','predicted_final_crater_km']):
  mid=q[q.heat_transfer_coefficient.eq(.01)]
  spread=q[q.heat_transfer_coefficient.gt(0)].groupby('elevation_km')[var].agg(['min','max'])
  ax.plot(mid.elevation_km,mid[var],color=col,label=f'{p:g} bar',lw=2 if p==3 else 1.3)
  ax.fill_between(spread.index,spread['min'],spread['max'],color=col,alpha=.09)
axs[0].set(xlabel='Elevation (km)',ylabel='Surviving projectile diameter (m)',title='Single-body ablation sensitivity')
axs[1].set(xlabel='Elevation (km)',ylabel='Final crater diameter (km)',title='Associated crater scaling')
for ax in axs:ax.grid(alpha=.16)
axs[1].legend(frameon=False,ncol=2)
fig.suptitle('250 m stony projectile at 12 km s⁻¹ and 45° entry\n3% H₂; shading spans assumed heat-transfer coefficients 0.005–0.03',y=1.05,fontsize=17)
fig.tight_layout();save(fig,'F4_Drag_Ablation_Sensitivity')

from pathlib import Path
import os,io
def put(dest,data):
 dest=Path(dest);temp=dest.with_name(dest.name+'.pending')
 with temp.open('wb') as f:
  f.write(data);f.flush();os.fsync(f.fileno())
 temp.replace(dest)
 assert dest.stat().st_size==len(data)
def install():
 import pandas as pd
 original=pd.DataFrame.to_csv
 def atomic(self,path_or_buf=None,*args,**kwargs):
  if not isinstance(path_or_buf,(str,Path)):return original(self,path_or_buf,*args,**kwargs)
  data=original(self,None,*args,**kwargs).encode();put(path_or_buf,data)
 pd.DataFrame.to_csv=atomic
 import matplotlib.figure
 save=matplotlib.figure.Figure.savefig
 def savefig(self,fname,*args,**kwargs):
  if not isinstance(fname,(str,Path)):return save(self,fname,*args,**kwargs)
  buf=io.BytesIO();kw=dict(kwargs);kw.setdefault('format',Path(fname).suffix.lstrip('.'));save(self,buf,*args,**kw);put(fname,buf.getvalue())
 matplotlib.figure.Figure.savefig=savefig
 write_text=Path.write_text
 def text(self,data,encoding=None,errors=None,newline=None):
  if newline is not None:data=data.replace('\n',newline)
  put(self,data.encode(encoding or 'utf-8',errors or 'strict'));return len(data)
 Path.write_text=text

from atomic_io import install
install()
from pathlib import Path
import pandas as pd,numpy as np,json,base64
B=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1];O=B/'work_v5/results'
tab=pd.read_csv(O/'full27_all_240_scenario_fits.csv')
a=np.load(O/'full27_all_sampled_predictions.npz')
states=[]
for i,r in tab.iterrows():
 s=r.to_dict();s['curve']=a['prediction'][i,int(r.sample_id)].tolist();s['valid']=bool(np.isfinite(a['prediction'][i,int(r.sample_id)]).all());states.append(s)
def clean(x):
 if isinstance(x,dict):return {k:clean(v) for k,v in x.items()}
 if isinstance(x,list):return [clean(v) for v in x]
 if isinstance(x,float) and not np.isfinite(x):return None
 return x
payload=json.dumps(clean({'states':states,'z':a['elevation'].tolist(),'obs':a['observed'].tolist(),'sd':a['sd'].tolist()}),allow_nan=False)
html="""<!doctype html><html lang="en"><meta charset="utf-8"><meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1"><title>Fresh crater atmospheric fits v5</title><style>
body{font:16px system-ui,sans-serif;line-height:1.55;margin:0;background:#f6f7f8;color:#182532}main{max-width:1120px;margin:auto;padding:30px}h1,h2{line-height:1.2}section{background:white;padding:24px;margin:22px 0;border:1px solid #d5dce1}label{display:inline-block;margin:10px 18px 10px 0}select{font:inherit;padding:7px}canvas{width:100%;height:auto}table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%}td,th{padding:10px;border:1px solid #d5dce1;text-align:left}img{width:100%;height:auto}small{color:#485766}a{color:#246880}</style>
<main><h1>Fresh crater atmospheric fits</h1><p>Richard Archer · Atlas AstroBioTek · v2310CT v5</p><p>Explore the archived entry surrogate against all 27 published fresh-crater relief means. The surrogate includes drag and a heuristic survival weight, but no ablation. Pressure weights and fits are conditional on its assumptions. The separate drag–ablation calculations are provided below and in the CSV archive.</p>
<section><h2>Choose a modeled state</h2><label>Pressure <select id="p"></select></label><label>H₂ percent <select id="h"></select></label><label>Humidity <select id="rh"></select></label><label>Material analog <select id="m"></select></label>
<canvas id="chart" width="1080" height="440"></canvas><p>Points: Archer (2023) Table 4 means ±1 SD. Curves: best sampled prediction for the selected state. All panels use the same relief scale.</p><table id="metrics"></table><p><small>Negative predictive R² means worse agreement than an elevation-invariant group mean. A high pooled R² can be driven by differences between crater-size groups. Fresh morphology does not establish Noachian formation age.</small></p></section>
<section><h2>Pressure sensitivity</h2><p>The historical 24-mean grid gives 3 bar 58.5% weight; all 27 means give 64.0%. Correct molar gas mixing raises the nominal weight to 72.6%, but using full observed SD in the likelihood flattens it to 20.7%. These are scenario sensitivities, not measured atmospheric probabilities.</p></section>
FIGURES
<section><h2>Provenance and references</h2><p>Archer (2023), Icarus 406, 115734. <a href="https://doi.org/10.1016/j.icarus.2023.115734">Source DOI</a>. (2026), <a href="https://doi.org/10.1038/s41467-026-76978-3">Nature Communications 17, 9790</a>. That study does not support categorical immunity to early impact sterilization.</p></section></main>
<script>const data=PAYLOAD;
const ids=['p','h','rh','m'],keys=['pressure_bar','h2_pct','relative_humidity','meteorite_class'];
ids.forEach((id,i)=>{let el=document.getElementById(id);[...new Set(data.states.map(s=>s[keys[i]]))].forEach(v=>{let o=document.createElement('option');o.value=v;o.textContent=v;el.appendChild(o)});el.onchange=draw});

draw();</script></html>"""
figures=''
for key in ['F2_RSquared_and_Pressure_Weights','F3_Actual_Diameter_Elevation','F4_Drag_Ablation_Sensitivity','F5_Pressure_Composition','F6_Tharsis_Budget']:
